# Tidelyne forensic audit log

Purpose: a permanent record of every finding raised by the forensic audit loop (one lens per auditor, one fixer per lens, one merger per round), with the fix that shipped and its status. Every finding is logged, including the ones that were deferred or could not be reproduced, so later rounds never re-discover the same issue without seeing what was decided. Findings JSON and the auditors' evidence live in the session scratchpad; the code is on main.

Column legend: **ID** — lens-scoped finding id (R<round>-<LENS>-<n>) · **Page** — hash route(s) or file the finding applies to · **Severity** — the auditor's rating (high / medium / low) · **Finding** — the auditor's one-line summary · **Fix applied** — what the fixer changed (and how the merger reconciled overlapping fixes) · **Status** — fixed / partial / deferred / not-reproduced.

## Round 1 — 2026-09-18

Round focus: first full sweep of the 3.1.1 site across six lenses — content & copy, visual & layout, function & data integrity, accessibility, code quality/performance/SEO, and swimming-domain accuracy — every page in the inventory, the nav/drawer/palette, the auth overlay, the legal pages, 404, sitemap, manifest and the service worker. Fixes were made on six branches (forensic/r1-*) and merged into main with conflicts resolved to keep every lens's intent; overlapping findings are cross-referenced in the Fix column. Post-merge smoke: check_index.py PASS, node --check on viz-human.js and sw.js, version strings untouched (3.1.1), all 22 pages open without console errors, the 3D lab builds and all five strokes render, drawer and palette open/close, backup v2 round-trips, calculators and log validation verified in the browser.

### Function & data (function) — 22 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1-FUNCTION-01 | My PRs (Import Backup) → affects every page | high | Importing a backup with a non-object log entry breaks the whole app on every later load | readLog() now drops non-object entries, normalises ids to numbers and writes the repaired array back; importBackup routes the log through a new sanitizeLogEntries() (plain objects with a real YYYY-MM-DD date, coerced numeric fields, capped text, ids assigned); the top-level initProfile() and renderLog() calls are wrapped in try/catch so stored data can never abort the app script. Verified: with swimiq_log='[null]' the page now loads with initTimers/initPlanner/initAnalytics/openPalette defined, nav bundle loaded, What's New populated, and the stored log repaired to []. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-02 | Training Log (data from Import Backup) | medium | Log entries from a backup file are injected into HTML/onclick without escaping (self-XSS) | renderLog coerces dist/dur/effort/id with Number() before interpolating, and the delete button is a data-del-id attribute handled by one delegated click listener on #log-entries (no inline onclick built from stored ids). Verified: the crafted entry renders as text (window.__pwned/__pwned2 stay undefined), and the delegated delete removes the entry. Merged with R1-A11Y-06 and R1-CODE-24: one .log-del button with data-del-id (delegated click), aria-label "Delete session <date> <type>" and CSS hover/focus colouring. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-03 | Calculator › Race Splits | medium | "Surge Middle" split strategy does not add up to the target time | calcSplits builds a zero-mean offsets array for every strategy, so the cumulative column always equals the target. Verified: 200 @ 2:00 surge and 1500 @ 16:00 surge both end at exactly the target. Merged with R1-DOMAIN-03: the calculator now uses the planner's buildSplits (single source of truth) with a renormalised surge pattern; verified every strategy sums exactly to the target for 100/200/1500. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-04 | Calculator › Race Splits | medium | Negative/positive split ramp is a fixed 0.4 s per 50 regardless of distance, giving impossible splits for long events | Negative/positive ramps are 4% of the average 50 from first to last (a 200 keeps its 1.2 s spread) and every 50 is clamped to >= 15 s. Verified: 1500 @ 16:00 negative split is now 32.64 -> 31.36 instead of 37.80 -> 26.20. Merged with R1-DOMAIN-03: the ±2 % half-difference ramp from buildSplits replaces the 4 % ramp (1500 @ 16:00 negative → 31.27 … 32.6 s per 50). | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-05 | every page (skip link at top of body) | medium | "Skip to content" link sends the user to the Home page | activatePage's guard now recognises an in-page anchor (the skip link's #main): it focuses the element with tabindex=-1 and restores the current view's hash via history.replaceState instead of bouncing to Home. Verified on #taper: after activating the skip link the hash is #taper, page-taper stays active, focus is on #main. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-06 | Training Log / Analytics | medium | Training log silently discards the oldest session after 100 entries | Log cap raised from 100 to LOG_MAX = 2000 (save and import), and the Training Log intro states the limit and points to Export Backup. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-07 | Training Log | medium | Log form accepts negative distance, absurd duration and effort outside 1–10 | saveLogEntry validates date, distance (0-100,000 m), duration (0-600 min) and effort (blank or an integer 1-10) with an inline role=alert message (#log-form-err), aria-invalid + focus on the offending field; the inputs carry min/max/step; sanitizeLogEntries applies the same caps on import. Verified: -500 / 99999 / 99 each produce the right message and nothing is saved until corrected. Merged with R1-CODE-23: the validation messages go through tlNotice("log-notice") (the separate #log-form-err element was dropped) and the success notice reads "Session saved — N logged." | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-08 | My PRs (backup) — affects Planner, Timers, Analytics | medium | Export/Import Backup does not include planner, timer or analytics data | exportBackup is version 2 and includes a validated tl object with tl_goals, tl_meet, tl_pred_fit, tl_split_last, tl_tempo, tl_interval, tl_paceclock and tl_an_prefs; importBackup restores those keys (plain objects only, allowlisted), keeps v1 files importable and reloads once so the timer/planner modules pick the settings up. Verified: v2 import restored 3 settings (bogus key and array value ignored), tempo showed 72 spm after reload, export contained the tl block. Merged with R1-COPY-07 (same schema; import feedback now goes through tlNotice and reloads after 1.5 s when settings were restored). | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-09 | My PRs | medium | PR fields accept any text with no validation or feedback | savePR parses the value with parseTime: invalid text is not stored, the input gets aria-invalid (red border) and a shared #pr-hint status shows 'Use ss.ss or m:ss.ss'; on change a valid value is normalised (52.3 -> 52.30); clearing the field removes the key. Verified for 'fast', '1:75', '52.3' and blank. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-10 | Planner › Race Predictor | medium | Race predictor's "fit your own exponent" accepts physically impossible pairs | runFit rejects fitted exponents below 1.0 ('Your longer swim is faster per 100 than your shorter one...') and above 1.4 before saving. Verified: 52.30 + 1:30.00 is rejected and tl_pred_fit stays empty; 52.30 + 1:55.00 fits (b = 1.137); 4:00.00 is rejected as implausible. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-11 | My PRs | low | Import accepts files that are not Tidelyne backups and reports success | importBackup requires a plain object with app === 'Tidelyne' and a numeric version >= 1, accepts only pr_ keys that map to PR_EVENTS with parseTime-valid values, and reports restored/skipped counts. Verified: [1,2,3] and {app:'Other'} are rejected; pr_evil and a numeric pr_50_FR are skipped while pr_100_FR '52.30' is restored ('1 PR, 1 session restored. 6 unreadable items were skipped.'). | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-12 | Calculator › Pace Zones (and everywhere parseTime is used) | low | parseTime accepts "1.55" as 1.55 s (contradicting its own comment) and Pace Zones has no plausibility check | parseTime now rejects a bare decimal under 10 s (so '1.55' is a typo, matching its comment); calcPaceZones requires 35 s-4:00 and, for an input shaped like m.ss, suggests the m:ss form. Verified: parseTime('1.55') === null, '5' and '20' alert with clear messages, '1:05.5' renders the table. Merged with R1-CODE-23 (messages via tlNotice("pace-notice")). | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-13 | router | low | Unknown hashes stay in the address bar and history | Unknown hashes are cleaned from the address bar with history.replaceState before Home is shown. Verified: '#nonexistent' -> page-home with location.hash ''. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-14 | Calculator, Timers (split taker) | low | Time formatters print "60:00" instead of "1:00:00" at one hour | formatTime and fmtSW gained an hours branch. Verified: formatTime(3600) = '1:00:00.00', fmtSW(3599.99,1) = '1:00:00.0'. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-15 | Planner › Split Planner | low | Split planner keeps the previous table on screen when a new run errors | runSplits uses a fail() helper on every error path that resets #pl-sp-out to the placeholder, hides the Copy button, clears the comparison and lastSplit. Verified: a valid 500 y plan followed by a 0:30 goal leaves 0 rows, the placeholder text, Copy hidden and an empty comparison. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-16 | 3D Visualizer | low | 3D lab view buttons never indicate the current view | View buttons carry data-view and aria-pressed; setView3D marks the current one (markView3D) and a manual orbit/zoom (mousedown/touchstart/wheel on the canvas while controls are enabled) clears it. Verified in the 3D lab. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-17 | Timers › Interval Timer | low | Interval timer cues are scheduled only inside the rAF frame, so a backgrounded/locked screen never beeps | While running, the interval timer also places cues from a 250 ms setInterval (itTick) with a 1.5 s lookahead when the document is hidden, finishes the circuit, and renders the display; keyed cues never double-fire. Verified with the pane hidden: 3 s after Start the display read 'GET READY / 7 / Elapsed 0:03' instead of the stale 'READY'. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-18 | 404.html | low | 404.html redirects with a relative path, which fails for nested URLs on GitHub Pages | 404.html derives the site root from the first path segment (/JAC_Online/ on github.io, / on a custom domain) for the redirect, the button and the favicon, with a comment for a future rename. Verified on the local server (root '/'). Merged with R1-CODE-01 and R1-A11Y-22: 404.html derives the root from the hostname (project folder on *.github.io), waits 20 s and offers "Stay on this page". | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-19 | Videos, 3D Visualizer demos | low | Console warning "Unrecognized feature: 'web-share'" every time a video is played | web-share removed from the youtube-nocookie iframe allow list. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-20 | AI Coach | low | AI Coach has no nav item, so nothing in the nav is marked current on #ai | The AI Coach button has id nav-ai-coach-btn; activatePage toggles .active and aria-current='page' on it for #ai (styled with a cyan ring). Verified on #ai and cleared on #home. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-21 | Workout Generator | low | Workout generator notes field is unbounded and pasted straight into the prompt | gen-notes has maxlength=300 and the value is trimmed and sliced to 300 before it enters the prompt. | fixed |
| R1-FUNCTION-22 | What's New (update toast) / offline | low | Service worker: "previous version" comes from unsorted cache keys, and query-string navigations are cached as separate entries | sw.js sorts stale cache versions with a numeric compareVersions before choosing 'previous', and networkFirst stores page responses under origin+pathname (pageKey) so query-string navigations share one entry. Verified with node (sort order, key normalisation, node --check); the pane cannot register service workers. Merged with R1-CODE-07/08/09: shellKey() keys shell navigations under INDEX and every other page by origin+pathname; cmpVer picks the newest stale cache. | fixed |

### Accessibility (a11y) — 25 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1-A11Y-01 | technique, videos, sets, dryland, visualizer, workouts, c… | high | 20 of 22 views have no page heading; titles are <div class="section-title"> | All 23 <div class="section-title"> page titles are now real headings: the first on each view is <h1 class="section-title"> (h2 on home, where the hero h1 already exists; also h2 for the second titles on visualizer/season). .section-title pins font-weight:400 so the switch from div to h1/h2 keeps the exact Bebas Neue rendering (zones' h1 was the only one rendering synthetic bold; it now matches). enhanceA11y additionally exposes the styled h3/h4 blocks at the next level down via role=heading/aria-level (rank compression per view, footer excluded) so every view reads h1 > h2 > h3 with no skipped level; verified in the browser: 22/22 views start at h1, 0 skipped levels. Merged with R1-CODE-05 (identical change; every view now has exactly one h1). | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-02 | home | high | Home feature cards are role=button with a 343-character name and headings swallowed | Home feature cards are plain containers (.card.card-clickable, position:relative) with a real <a href="#page" class="card-link"> inside the <h3>; .card-link::after covers the card so the whole card is still clickable (inset 0 verified), :has(.card-link:focus-visible) draws the site ring around the card. Removed the inline onclick/cursor styles and dropped .card[onclick] from enhanceA11y; the 6 h3s are back in the heading tree, 0 role=button cards remain, link name = title only. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-03 | profile (My PRs) | high | 18 PR inputs have no label (placeholder "—" only) | buildPRGrid renders <label class="pr-event" for="pr-in-{key}"> + <input id="pr-in-{key}" aria-describedby="pr-format-hint">; one sr-only hint on the profile page explains the m:ss.hh format. pr_* storage keys untouched. Verified: 0 unlabeled PR inputs (was 18), label '50 FR' resolves. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-04 | technique, videos, sets, visualizer, calculator, nutritio… | high | Tabs: role=tab without aria-controls, no role=tabpanel, no roving tabindex, unnamed tablists | enhanceA11y now implements the full tabs pattern: tablist aria-label from the view's .section-title (or data-label), each tab gets id/aria-controls parsed from its switchTab onclick, each .tab-panel gets role=tabpanel + aria-labelledby (tabindex=0 only when it has no focusable content), roving tabindex 0/-1 (also maintained in switchTab, including the planner's programmatic switch), plus Home/End keys. Verified: 63/63 tabs wired, 63 labelled tabpanels, 13/13 tablists named, ArrowRight/End/Home move focus and selection. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-05 | technique (16), dryland, calculator, nutrition, race, tap… | medium | Accordion headers: <h4> nested inside role=button, no aria-controls | enhanceA11y moves each accordion title into a real <button type=button class=acc-btn aria-expanded aria-controls=acc-body-N> inside the h4 (font/letter-spacing inherited, so it renders identically); the header div no longer gets role=button/tabindex, and toggleAcc resolves closest('.acc-header') and updates aria-expanded on the button. Clicks still bubble to the header's onclick, so the palette's openAcc path works unchanged. Verified: 18 buttons with matching bodies, 0 headings inside buttons, toggling syncs aria-expanded. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-06 | log | medium | Log-entry delete button has no accessible name and is 21×30 px | Log delete is <button type=button class=log-del aria-label="Delete session {date} {type}"> with the glyph aria-hidden; .log-del gives a 44x44 target and the red state on :hover and :focus-visible (inline onmouseover/onmouseout removed). Verified name 'Delete session 2026-09-10 Aerobic', min-height 44px. Merged with R1-FUNCTION-02 / R1-CODE-24. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-07 | all (Log In / Sign Up dialog) | medium | Auth-gate error messages are never announced | login-error/signup-error carry role=alert, forgot-msg role=status aria-live=polite; showAuthError marks that screen's inputs aria-invalid=true + aria-describedby=<message id>, and showAuthScreen clears both. Verified live: after showAuthError('login') the email field has aria-invalid/aria-describedby=login-error, cleared on screen change. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-08 | home (any deep link) | medium | On first load focus is moved to the page body, so the skip link is the 25th Tab stop | activatePage(name, opts) skips the focus move when opts.focus===false; the DOMContentLoaded call passes it, hashchange navigations still move focus. Verified: fresh deep-link load of #technique leaves focus on <body> (skip link is the first Tab stop), subsequent hash navigation focuses page-sets/page-season. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-09 | visualizer | medium | Visualizer phase steps are mouse-only, 8.3 px text, low-contrast | Phase steps render as <button type=button class=phase-step aria-pressed>; updatePhaseBar3D keeps aria-pressed in sync; the bar is role=group 'Stroke phases'. .phase-step is now 0.62rem (9.92px, was 8.3px) in --white3 with button chrome reset; mobile breakpoints raised from 0.44/0.55rem to 0.58/0.62rem. Verified at 1280px (six 149x13 steps in the 40px bar, no overflow) and 375px; buttons focusable, aria-pressed follows the active phase. Merged with R1-VISUAL-04. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-10 | ai, visualizer | medium | Same-state toggle groups lack aria-pressed (AI modes, viz speed) | aria-pressed added to the six AI mode buttons (not #mode-clear) and the three speed buttons, maintained in setMode() and setSpeed3D(). Verified initial states true/false and toggling. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-11 | sets (6 tables, 18 th), timers (3 tables, 15 th), race (1… | medium | Data tables have <th> without scope and no captions | scope="col" added to every header-row <th> in the 15 thead rows (static tables and the calculator's JS templates); enhanceA11y also sets scope on any thead th and names each caption-less table (aria-label) after its tab (sets tables → 'AeT Work', 'Threshold'…) or the nearest preceding heading; the two timers result tables that have no preceding heading got explicit aria-labels ('Send-off schedule', 'Recorded splits'). Verified: 0 th without scope (was 43), 17/17 tables named. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-12 | season (5 links), auth gate (Terms of Use, Privacy Policy) | medium | Links that open a new tab give no warning (season, auth gate) | New helper markNewTabLinks() appends an sr-only ' (opens in a new tab)' plus an aria-hidden ↗ glyph (skipped when the label already ends in an arrow) to every a[target=_blank]; run from enhanceA11y and again for the season view after renderSeasonPage so the dynamic meet links are covered. Verified: 0 unwarned new-tab links, season's 5 links (incl. 'TYR Futures Championships 2027') announce the new tab. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-13 | technique (8 tabs, 909 px of tabs in 335 px), injury (8),… | medium | Tab strips scroll horizontally with the scrollbar hidden — no affordance that more tabs exist | .tabs gets a CSS mask driven by --tabs-fl/--tabs-fr; enhanceA11y measures each strip (scroll listener + ResizeObserver, so hidden views re-measure when shown) and sets a 40px right fade while more tabs are off-screen and a 28px left fade once scrolled. Verified at 375px on technique (scrollWidth 881 vs 335): right fade on at start, off at the end with the left fade on, both mid-way; no fade when a strip fits. Merged with R1-VISUAL-02. | fixed |

Cards and accordions are now real links/buttons and inherit it anyway. Merged with R1-VISUAL-03. | fixed |
| R1-A11Y-15 | home, season (card tags), visualizer (`#motion-btn`) | low | Purple .card-tag and "AI Reconstruct" text fail 4.5:1 | Added --purple-text:#c084fc (≈6.4:1 on the card background) and used it for the three purple card tags and the #motion-btn 'AI Reconstruct' label; --purple stays for borders/glows. | R1-A11Y-17 | technique, videos, sets, visualizer, ai, nav | low | Decorative emoji in tab and button labels are read aloud | Leading emoji on the 13 tab buttons, the 7 AI mode buttons, the 5 visualizer stroke buttons and the AI Reconstruct button are wrapped in <span aria-hidden="true">; the trailing ✦ on 'AI Coach', 'Talk to AI Coach' and 'Ask AI Coach' likewise. | R1-A11Y-18 | home | low | Hero heading reads "TRAINELITE.SWIM FAST." | Whitespace between the three block spans; accessible name/copied text is now 'TRAIN ELITE. SWIM FAST.' and the spans still stack (display:block, three distinct line tops verified). | fixed |

### Code quality, performance & SEO (code) — 25 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1-CODE-01 | 404 | high | 404.html redirects to itself on nested paths (infinite loop) | 404.html computes the site root once (project folder on *.github.io, otherwise '/') and uses it for the button, the favicon and the 2.5 s redirect, skipping the redirect when already at root; theme-color/color-scheme added. Reproduced at /deep/path/ (button and redirect pointed at the 404 itself, tab re-navigated forever); after: button -> root, redirect lands on the app. Merged with R1-FUNCTION-18 and R1-A11Y-22 (20 s cancellable redirect instead of 2.5 s). | fixed |
| R1-CODE-02 | all | medium | initReveal() leaks one IntersectionObserver per route change | initReveal() now reuses one module-level IntersectionObserver (_revealObs). Reproduced: instrumented constructor; after the fix 45 activatePage() calls across all 22 routes construct 0 observers (was one per call) with the 3 pending .reveal elements still observed. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-03 | workouts, visualizer | medium | Server-returned error text is written to innerHTML unescaped (AI workout + AI viz) | Both innerHTML error sites (workout generator, AI visualizer) wrap err.error in escapeHTML(). | fixed |
| R1-CODE-04 | all | medium | Google Fonts stylesheet is render-blocking | Google Fonts CSS is <link rel=preload as=style> promoted to a stylesheet on load, with a <noscript> stylesheet fallback; renderBlockingStatus is now 'non-blocking' and Bebas Neue / DM Sans still load. All declared variants (300, 500, italic 300) are used, so none were dropped. Note: the preload link is promoted to rel=stylesheet on load, so a post-load DOM check sees rel="stylesheet". | fixed |
| R1-CODE-05 | every page except home and zones | medium | 20 of 22 views have no heading element; page titles are <div class="section-title"> | All 23 <div class=section-title> became headings: the page-leading one on 20 views is <h1>, the secondary ones (home, visualizer 'Ask the visualizer', season 'Futures/Juniors') are <h2>; .section-title gained font-weight:400 and margin-top:0 so Bebas Neue is not synthetically bolded. Every page now reports exactly one h1 (home keeps its hero h1). Merged with R1-A11Y-01. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-06 | all | medium | supabase-js is loaded from a floating major tag with no pin or SRI | supabase-js pinned to @2.116.0/dist/umd/supabase.js (the packaged file, not jsDelivr's dynamic .min.js which its own banner says must not carry SRI) with integrity sha384 computed locally and cross-checked against jsDelivr's published sha256 for that file, crossorigin=anonymous. Page loads with supabase.createClient available. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-07 | PWA | low | sw.js stores the app shell up to three times and precaches files the app never requests | sw.js PRECACHE drops './', './404.html' and './og.png'; networkFirst stores every shell navigation ('/', '/?x', '/index.html') under the single INDEX key via shellKey() and looks it up the same way offline (privacy.html etc. keep their own keys). Verified with a Node harness. Merged with R1-FUNCTION-22 (shellKey also keys non-shell pages by origin+pathname). | fixed |
| R1-CODE-08 | visualizer / PWA | low | Versioned viz-human.js?v= is cached beside the precached un-versioned copy | staleWhileRevalidate keys cache-busted requests (?v=) by origin+pathname (assetKey) for both match and put, so ./viz-human.js?v=3.1.1 shares the precached entry; the loader's hard-coded '3.1.1' fallback is gone (window.TL_VERSION \|\| ''). | fixed |
| R1-CODE-09 | updates / PWA | low | sw.js reports an arbitrary "previous" version | sw.js picks the semantically newest stale cache via a cmpVer copy (harness: 3.0.1/3.1.0/2.9.9 -> 3.1.0) instead of the last key caches.keys() happened to return. Merged with R1-FUNCTION-22 (one cmpVer; the duplicate compareVersions was dropped). | fixed |
| R1-CODE-10 | all (print) | low | Print stylesheet hides elements that do not exist; the real toast/drawer print | Print block now hides #tl-upd-toast/.tl-upd-toast, #tl-palette, #nav-drawer, .nav-scrim and .tl-pal-navbtn (the real toast, palette and drawer); the unreferenced .nav-scroll, .grid-4 and .tech-img-* rules are deleted. Verified in CSSOM. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-11 | n/a | low | Comments cite files that are not in the repo | All 15 comments citing script.js, NOTES.md, VIDEO_PLAN.md, apply_facades.py and check_index.py are reworded to the inline modules; README.md gains a 'Maintainer notes' section that houses the season-calendar, predictor-exponent, pacing-pattern, intensity-reference and duplicate-id notes the comments now point to. Merged with R1-COPY-16/17. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-12 | n/a | low | Duplicate helper implementations across modules; no global lsGet | Global lsGet() added beside lsSet/lsRemove; the planner and What's New lsGet, analytics anLsGet and the three esc/anEsc copies now delegate to the app helpers; openPalette() closes the nav drawer first so the drawer's and the palette's body-scroll locks never stack (verified at 375 px: drawer closed, body lock cleared, focus back on the hamburger after closing the palette). The two setText/fmtDate pairs were left alone because they intentionally differ in behaviour. The dupcheck heuristic now counts three column-0 "lsGet" declarations; two are IIFE-local shims (brace depth 1) that delegate to the new global, verified in the browser. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-13 | videos/technique | low | Dead DEMO_CLIPS feature and its 40 lines of loader code | The dead DEMO_CLIPS map, initDemoClips, setClip and their DOMContentLoaded hook (42 lines) are removed; nothing else referenced them. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-14 | visualizer | low | console.info fires on every swimmer build | The swimmer-build console.info is gated on window.TL_DEBUG \|\| lsGet('tl_debug')==='1'. Verified: a full build ran (58k triangles) with no 'swimmer built' console entry. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-15 | all | low | Native form controls and scrollbars are not told the page is dark | <meta name=color-scheme content=dark> plus color-scheme:dark on :root (also on the 404 and legal pages); computed colorScheme is 'dark' so date pickers, selects, scrollbars and autofill render dark. | fixed |
| R1-CODE-16 | privacy, terms, disclaimer | low | Legal pages have no theme-color / manifest / apple-touch-icon | privacy/terms/disclaimer heads gain theme-color, color-scheme, SVG+PNG favicons, apple-touch-icon and the manifest link; verified in the browser. | fixed |

**Round 1 totals:** 163 findings — 161 fixed, 0 partial, 2 deferred, 0 not reproduced. Post-merge fix commit: "forensic r1: post-merge fix" (calculator Zone 1 HR ceiling).

## Round 2 — 2026-09-18

Round focus: second full sweep of 3.1.1 after the round-1 merge (a836a04), with each lens first re-checking every round-1 fix for regressions and then going deeper — every tab, accordion, dialog and dynamic render on all 22 hash routes at desktop, 768, 375 and 320 px; the calculators, log, backup, planner, timers and analytics driven with concrete inputs; the 3D lab measured per phase on the human rig; the service worker, CSP, SRI, fonts, sitemap, robots and the legal pages; the swimming facts re-verified against the primary sources. Fixes were made on six branches (forensic/r2-copy, -visual, -function, -a11y, -code, -domain) and merged into main one by one; 44 conflict hunks (visual 2, function 3, a11y 21, code 9 + viz-human.js 1, domain 10) were resolved by hand keeping every lens's intent — the cross-references are in the Fix column. Post-merge: check_index.py PASS, dupcheck against HEAD~6 (no new duplicate top-level names; 19 new names, 2 removed helpers with no remaining references), node --check viz-human.js and sw.js, version strings untouched (sw.js VERSION = UPDATES[0].version = footer = 3.1.1). Browser smoke of the merged site (own tab, served copy on :8655): all 22 pages open with no console errors beyond the pane's known service-worker registration failure and no CSP violations; every route title carries " — Tidelyne"; the 3D lab builds under the new SRI/CSP and all five strokes switch and render (skinned human rig in the scene), play/pause, model, X-ray, forces, view tweens and arrow-key phase stepping work; the drawer (52 px head, close button on the burger's box) and the ⌘K / "/" palette open, filter, route and close; calculators run through calcFail with aria-invalid/aria-describedby, sr-only captions and the 19 s split guard; Enter submits calculator, generator and log panels once; the log sorts by date, rejects future dates, hides the stale 30-day summary and re-fills today's date; PR fields debounce; backup export/import asks before overwriting and round-trips; the tempo trainer's spm↔cpm round trip restores 65; the auth dialog traps focus on the close button and re-opens cleanly after a fast close; the What's New card shows the merged service-worker message; privacy/terms/disclaimer/404 use the promoted font preload; sitemap.xml parses. Two post-merge fixes were committed ("forensic r2: post-merge fix"): the duplicated Enter-to-submit handler collapsed into one, and the last dead NOTES reference in viz-human.js trimmed.

### Function & data (function) — 15 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R2-FUNCTION-01 | log | medium | Training-log history is ordered by save time, not by session date | Added sortLogEntries (date desc, then id desc); saveLogEntry sorts before writing (new entry prepended so an equal-date tie keeps the newest save first) and renderLog sorts for display, so imported and after-the-fact sessions sit where they happened. Verified: 09-15, 09-01, 09-17 saves render 09-17, 09-15, 09-01. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-02 | profile (My PRs › Import Backup) | medium | Import Backup silently replaces the local log, PRs and name with no confirmation, and an empty name in the file wipes the saved name | importBackup now confirm()s before writing when the device holds data the file will overwrite, naming only what that file carries ('the 3 sessions, 2 PRs and saved settings already on this device'); declining leaves everything untouched with an 'Import cancelled' notice; swimmer_name is written only when d.name.trim() is non-empty. Verified: log:[] on a 3-session device is declined/kept; name:'' keeps 'Dan'; a name-only file asks nothing. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-03 | every page (Log in / Sign up overlay, AI gates) | medium | Auth dialog: closing then re-opening within 450 ms makes the re-opened dialog vanish; the invisible overlay eats clicks and blocks the palette during the fade | closeAuthGate stores its hide timer, adds a .closing class and pointer-events:none immediately; openAuthGate clears the timer, removes .closing, resets transition/opacity. New authGateVisible() drives _authTrap, and the palette's authGateOpen() treats .closing as closed. Verified: close then re-open at +100 ms is still visible at +550 ms (signup screen shown); during the fade elementFromPoint no longer hits the overlay and openPalette() opens. Merged with R2-CODE-02: openAuthGate first defers until the overlay markup exists (code), then clears the hide timer / .closing (function). | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-04 | log | medium | 30-day summary keeps showing stale numbers once no session falls in the last 30 days | renderLog hides #log-stats (else branch) when entries exist but none fall in the last 30 days, matching the empty-log behaviour. Verified: one 2026-05-01 session leaves the panel display:none instead of the stale '3 sessions · 12.0 km'. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-05 | calculator (all four tabs) | low | Calculator page keeps the previous result table on screen when a new run errors (Golf, Splits, Pace Zones, Converter) | Added calcFail(noticeId,msg,focusId,resultId) with CALC_PLACEHOLDERS: resets #split-result/#pace-result/#conv-result to their placeholder and removes .show from #golf-result before the notice; used on every early return in calcGolf/calcSplits (incl. the planner-unavailable path)/calcPaceZones/convertTime. Verified: blank after a valid run leaves 0 rows and the placeholder text; golf result loses .show. Merged with R2-A11Y-05: calcFail now calls tlNotice(noticeId,msg,null,focusId) so the offending field also gets aria-invalid + aria-describedby. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-06 | calculator, log, analytics | low | Enter does nothing in the Calculator, Training Log and Analytics forms (the Planner, auth and AI forms all submit on Enter) | One delegated keydown listener: plain Enter in a text/number/date input inside .calc-panel or the Log's .tl-panel (not in a <form>, not defaultPrevented, no modifiers) clicks the panel's .calc-btn or .btn-sm. Covers Calculator, Log, Analytics, Timers' interval panel and the planner fields without their own handler; planner fields with handlers (preventDefault) do not double-fire; textarea and Shift+Enter untouched. Verified in the browser for split-time, log-distance, an-sw-time, pl-sp-goal (single run), pl-sp-fade, tlt-so-reps. Merged with R2-A11Y-20 in the post-merge fix: the two delegated Enter handlers were collapsed into one top-level listener covering `.calc-panel`, `.gen-form` (#gen-btn) and the Log panel. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-07 | profile | low | PR fields flag "invalid" on the first keystroke and store half-typed values | savePR keeps a per-key timer (prErrTimers): on input both the invalid flag/hint and the store of a valid value wait for an 800 ms typing pause; change (blur) flushes at once with normalisation; a value that becomes valid clears a previous error immediately. Verified: typing 1,1:,1:5,1:52,1:52.,1:52.3 never sets aria-invalid or stores an intermediate; after the pause '1:52.3' is stored; change normalises to 1:52.30; 'abc' flags after 800 ms. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-08 | timers (Tempo Trainer) | low | Tempo trainer: toggling strokes/min → cycles/min → strokes/min changes the tempo by one | tlt_setMode remembers the pre-conversion tempo (TP.undo = {mode, bpm, at}) and restores it when switching straight back with no edit in between. Verified: 65 spm → 33 cpm → 65 spm; after editing to 34 cpm the switch back gives 68 (no stale restore). | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-09 | ai | low | AI Coach stacks identical bubbles: every logged-out Send adds another "needs a free account" message, and clicking the current mode again adds another "Mode: …" message | setMode returns early when the mode is already current; sendToAI's logged-out branch skips the gate bubble when the last AI bubble already is that text (lastAIBubbleText helper). Verified: 3 Sends logged out add 1 bubble; clicking the active chip twice adds 1 'Mode:' line; switching modes still announces. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-10 | technique (Ask the Visualizer form) | low | Technique › "Ask the Visualizer": the "Custom Description (optional)" text is ignored unless the concept dropdown is set to Custom, and it has no length cap | Prompt topic is the preset plus ' — focus: <text>' when text is given (Custom still uses the text alone); textarea gets maxlength=300 and the value is sliced to 300; label reads 'Extra focus (optional) — or your own concept for Custom'. Verified from the built function source and DOM. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-11 | log (and Import Backup) | low | Training log accepts sessions dated in the future, and the backup sanitiser accepts impossible dates like 2026-02-31 | Added parseLogDate (strict round-trip, same rule as planner/analytics) and logTodayISO(offset); saveLogEntry rejects unparseable dates and anything later than tomorrow with an inline message; sanitizeLogEntries applies the same rule to backups; #log-date gets max=tomorrow. Verified: 2027-01-01 and 2026-02-31 are refused in the form and dropped by the sanitiser; today and tomorrow save. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-12 | calculator (Golf Score) | low | Golf-score inputs' HTML min/max disagree with the JavaScript limits | GOLF_LIMITS {split:[20,120], strokes:[10,60]} now drives calcGolf's range checks and messages, matching the existing min/max attributes. Verified: 16 s / 8 strokes now yields '50m split should be between 20 and 120 seconds.' and no stale score. Merged with R2-A11Y-05 (field flag via calcFail) and R2-COPY-15 ("50 m" spacing in the messages). | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-13 | visualizer | low | 3D lab view presets disable orbit controls until a requestAnimationFrame tween finishes; a hidden tab or a second click mid-tween leaves controls off or two tweens fighting, and reduced-motion is ignored | setView3D keeps one _viewTween3D handle: cancelViewTween3D(finish) cancels a running tween (a second preset click replaces the first), document.hidden or prefers-reduced-motion jumps straight to the end pose with controls enabled, a tween that notices the tab went hidden finishes itself, and stopViz3DLoop finishes any running tween. Verified with the pane hidden (camera at the top pose, controls enabled) and with stubbed rAF/hidden for the two-click visible path. Merged with R2-CODE-08: stopViz3DLoop also cancels a running view tween; vizLabActive() gates the router callback and startViz3D. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-14 | updates | low | "Check for updates" blames https/browser support when the service worker simply failed to register on a supported host | New swFailed flag set in registerSW's catch paths; tlCheckForUpdates and the offline note show 'Offline support could not be set up in this browser session — reload to try again; the site still works online.' when registration failed on a supported host, keeping the https/browser-support wording only for unsupported hosts. Verified on the served localhost copy where sw.js registration fails. Merged with R2-CODE-17: the function lens's SW_FAILED_MSG constant is used by both the status card and the update check; the code lens's console.warn with the error is kept. | fixed |
| R2-FUNCTION-15 | log | low | Log form's default date is computed once at load and goes stale after midnight | setLogDateToday() fills #log-date with today's local date only when the field is empty or still holds the previous auto value (a typed date is kept) and sets max; called at load, from a new window.onShow_log hook and on visibilitychange (visible). Verified: user date 2026-09-10 kept; empty refilled; a stale auto value is replaced. | fixed |

### Accessibility (a11y) — 22 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R2-A11Y-01 | all (Log In / Sign Up / Reset dialog) | high | Regression of R1-A11Y-21 / R1-VISUAL-07: the auth-gate close button is unreachable by keyboard | _authTrap now filters focusables with !disabled && !hidden && getClientRects().length>0 (the fixed-position close button has no offsetParent); the close button is the first trap stop. | R2-A11Y-02 | analytics (Stroke rate & DPS tab), planner (Race Predictor tab) | high | Phone layout zooms out to half size on Analytics › Stroke rate & DPS and pans on Planner › Race Predictor | .calc-panel, .form-group and .form-input/.form-select/.form-textarea get min-width:0; .gen-row tracks are minmax(min(190px,100%),1fr). Verified at 375px: Analytics Stroke rate innerWidth 751->375, panels 731->335; Planner Race Predictor 391->375, panels 370->335; every page and tab fits at 375 and 320. See R2-COPY-01 for the merged CSS. | fixed |

### Code quality, performance & SEO (code) — 17 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R2-CODE-01 | SEO | high | sitemap.xml is not well-formed XML (regression of R1-CODE-17 / R1-COPY-15) | sitemap.xml: rewrote the round-1 comment without any '--' (points at README 'Releasing a new version' instead of quoting the git command); xmllint --noout and minidom now parse it. README release step 4 tells the releaser to run xmllint --noout sitemap.xml. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-02 | all (first load) | high | The deferred supabase-js script gates the app's first paint on a third-party CDN | supabase-js now loads with async (integrity/crossorigin kept) and reports through onload/onerror -> onSupabaseScript(), which creates the client as soon as the library lands (or on DOMContentLoaded, whichever is later); openAuthGate() queues a Log In click while the script is still in flight instead of alerting, and waits for the overlay markup if clicked mid-parse. Before/after with a 4 s stub CDN: domContentLoadedEventStart 4020 ms -> 27 ms (domInteractive 24 ms), the routed page paints immediately. See R2-FUNCTION-03 for the openAuthGate order. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-03 | auth overlay / router | high | Password-reset (and every other auth-callback) link is dead: the router strips the token hash before supabase-js can read it, and no recovery UI exists | Router keeps auth-callback hashes (isAuthCallbackHash: access_token/refresh_token/error*/type) so supabase-js can read them; initSupabaseAuth subscribes onAuthStateChange and PASSWORD_RECOVERY opens a new screen-newpass card (password + confirm, 8+ chars, sb.auth.updateUser, then signed-in close); #error_description=... opens the login/forgot card with the decoded message; a rejected recovery token opens the forgot card with 'could not be verified'; the URL is cleaned in every branch and onAuthSuccess no longer closes the overlay while recovery is pending. Verified: the fake recovery hash now reaches /auth/v1/user (previously zero requests), expired-link hash shows the message, simulated PASSWORD_RECOVERY opens the new-password screen with focus in the field. Owner still has to confirm the site URL is in the Supabase redirect allow-list (backend read-only). | fixed |
| R2-CODE-04 | visualizer | medium | three.min.js (603 KB) is injected from jsDelivr with no integrity hash or crossorigin | ensureViz3DReady sets s.crossOrigin='anonymous' and s.integrity='sha384-CI3ELBVUz9XQO+97x6nwMDPosPR5XvsxW2ua7N1Xeygeh1IxtgqtCkGfQY9WWdHu' (hash recomputed from the fetched 603,445-byte file); the lab loaded, executed and rendered under it in the browser. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-05 | all | medium | No Content-Security-Policy at all | Added <meta http-equiv=Content-Security-Policy> (default-src 'self'; script-src self+unsafe-inline+cdn.jsdelivr.net; style-src self+unsafe-inline+fonts.googleapis.com; font-src self+fonts.gstatic.com; img-src self data: i.ytimg.com; connect-src self+the Supabase project; frame-src youtube-nocookie; worker-src/manifest-src self; object-src none; base-uri/form-action self). Smoke-tested: Google Fonts, supabase-js + Three.js from jsDelivr, Supabase connect (auth health 200), YouTube thumbnail + facade iframe, WebGL lab render, blob backup download, palette, every route; a fetch to example.com is refused. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-06 | PWA | medium | sw.js precaches viz-human.js (330 KB) for a 3D lab that can never run offline, plus an asset nothing references | sw.js: logo-full.svg dropped from PRECACHE and the unreferenced file deleted (tools/make_logo.py can regenerate it); favicon.ico added; the pinned three.min.js is now served cache-first from a separate 'tl-vendor-cache' that survives version sweeps (stale entries for other Three builds are swept on activate), so the 3D lab works offline once opened; viz-human.js stays precached so the lab is offline-capable after an update too. What's New status copy updated accordingly. A Node harness verified cache-first, offline serving, opaque responses uncached, other CDN URLs untouched and the activate sweep keeping the vendor cache (the harness caught and fixed a prefix collision with 'tidelyne-v'). | fixed |
| R2-CODE-07 | PWA / updates | low | Update flow: skipWaiting + clients.claim() lets the old page load the new viz-human.js | Page-side guard (option 2): the updates module sets window.TL_SHELL_STALE when the controlling worker's version is newer than window.TL_VERSION (markShellStale on TL_UPDATED/TL_VERSION) and exposes window.tlShowUpdateToast(note); loadHumanScripts then shows the mannequin and the toast 'Tidelyne updated to vX — reload to open the updated 3D lab' instead of injecting a module built for the newer shell. Verified by simulation (no script injected, toast shown). | fixed |
| R2-CODE-08 | visualizer → any | low | Visualizer render loop starts (and keeps running) on other pages when the lab is left within 30 ms or while Three.js is still downloading | Added vizLabActive(); the router's 30 ms callback returns early when the lab is no longer active, and startViz3D builds the scene but only calls animate3D() when the lab is on screen (the router starts the loop on the next visit). Verified: quick hop -> _raf3D 0 and no Three.js download; leaving during the download -> scene initialised with _raf3D 0; returning starts the loop, leaving stops it. See R2-FUNCTION-13. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-09 | profile (My PRs) | low | Backup/PR/profile code still reads localStorage raw, bypassing the lsGet helper R1-CODE-12 added | buildPRGrid, exportBackup, initProfile and the palette's readRecent read through lsGet; exportBackup's whole body is inside its try so a storage failure surfaces via tlNotice. Verified with Storage.prototype.getItem throwing: initProfile and exportBackup no longer throw and the PR grid still builds. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-10 | n/a | low | Dead code and overridden rules left in the page | Removed seriesLabel, the .goal-input selector and .badge.purple; moved #8ba5bd/#7b95ad into the primary :root and deleted the override block; reduced the two IIFE shims to lsPut(k,v){return !!window.lsSet(k,v)} / lsDel(k){window.lsRemove(k)}. See R2-VISUAL-10. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-11 | visualizer | low | R1-CODE-11 leftover: a comment still cites the non-existent preview/NOTES.md | viz-human.js comment now reads '(index.html updateSwimmer; see README.md "Maintainer notes")' — no motion values touched; grep NOTES.md finds nothing. See R2-COPY-24. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-12 | privacy, terms, disclaimer, 404 | low | Legal pages and 404 still load Google Fonts render-blocking (R1-CODE-04 fixed index.html only) | privacy/terms/disclaimer/404 use the preload-and-promote pattern with a <noscript> fallback, and request only DM Sans 400;600 (the weights the pages use; <strong> resolves to 600 as before). Verified the link is promoted to rel=stylesheet and Bebas Neue / DM Sans 400+600 load. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-13 | all | low | No /favicon.ico | Generated favicon.ico (32 px + 16 px classic BMP entries from the same mark), taught tools/make_pwa_icons.py to regenerate it, added <link rel=icon href=favicon.ico sizes=32x32> (listed first so browsers keep the SVG/PNG they show today) to index.html and the legal pages, the /JAC_Online/ form on 404.html whose ROOT rewrite now covers every icon link, and added it to the SW PRECACHE. Note: on GitHub Pages the origin-root /favicon.ico is outside this repo; the file answers /JAC_Online/favicon.ico and the link tag. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-14 | SEO / hygiene | low | Backend sources, tool scripts and (after the next push) the audit log are published on the public site | robots.txt now disallows /supabase/, /tools/, /README.md and /LICENSE (with a comment); README's file table says the folders are published but disallowed. The longer-term Actions/docs/ deploy change was not made (out of scope). | fixed |
| R2-CODE-15 | all | low | Bebas Neue swaps in with no size-adjusted fallback, so the hero and every section title reflow on font arrival | Three metric-matched @font-face fallbacks (Bebas Fallback: Arial Narrow 71%; Bebas Fallback Impact: Impact 76%; Bebas Fallback Sans: Arial/Roboto/Helvetica 59%; all ascent 90% / descent 30% / line-gap 0) chained in --fh before sans-serif. Measured in the browser: sample width 1244 px vs 1245 px with Bebas Neue (plain sans-serif: 1998 px); at 375 px the hero h1 stays 3 lines and the whole Home page height is identical (5733 px) with the fallback and with Bebas Neue, versus 5894 px with plain sans-serif. See R2-A11Y-08. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-16 | all hash routes | low | Route titles omit the brand and two exceed 60 characters | setPageTitle appends ' — Tidelyne' to every non-home route; shortened calculator, planner, technique, sets, timers and analytics titles and dropped the duplicated brand from the updates entry so every full title is <= 60 characters (longest now exactly 60). Verified titles on 6 routes. See R2-COPY-07: the copy lens's setPageTitle rule shipped; the shortened PAGE_TITLES entries and the de-branded updates title are kept. | fixed |
| R2-CODE-17 | updates | low | What's New blames "needs https / no service-worker support" when registration itself failed | Separate swFailed flag set in register().catch (and the outer catch, with a console.warn carrying the error); the status card now says 'Offline support could not be set up in this browser (the service worker failed to install). Reload to retry; the site still works online.' and tlCheckForUpdates has a matching message, reserving the https/support sentence for !canRegister(). ### Swimming domain accuracy (domain) — 18 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R2-DOMAIN-01 | visualizer | medium | Freestyle and backstroke are authored with true opposition timing (R[i] = mirror of L[i+3]), so phases i and i+3 are the SAME body configuration mirrored — yet they carry different physics. Measured on the human rig: freestyle phase 3 "Pull" has the L hand at (0.15, 0.14, −0.30) m mid-pull and phase 6 "Reach Forward" has the R hand at (0.15, 0.19, +0.35) — identical — but the panel reads **142 N thrust / 2.18 m/s / net +76** vs **24 N / 2.02 / net −6**. Backstroke phase 1 "Pinky Entry" (R hand finishing at the hip, (−0.04, 0.19, −0.44)) reads **11 N / 1.55 m/s / −12** while its mirror phase 4 "In-Sweep to Hip" reads **130 N / 2.05 / +68**. The description of phase 6 even says "the opposite arm drives through the middle of its pull" while the thrust box shows 24 N. A coach stepping through the phases sees the swimmer slow from 2.05 to 1.55 m/s twice a cycle in backstroke for no visible reason. | index.html STROKES: freestyle pairs (0,3)/(1,4)/(2,5) now carry identical physics 44/36/16/1.94/66, 40/28/24/1.84/62, 60/80/40/1.80/74 (weighted net +2.1 N); backstroke pairs 40/34/12/1.86/62, 38/24/20/1.76/58, 56/78/30/1.72/68 (+2.6 N). Verified on the human rig: fs phase 3 L hand (0.15,0.14,-0.30) and phase 6 R hand (0.15,0.19,0.35) both read 80 N / 60 N / +20 / 1.80; bk phases 1 and 4 both 34/40/-6/1.86. 'In-Sweep to Hip' desc no longer claims peak thrust (it is the finish, mirror of the entry). See R2-COPY-16. | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-02 | technique (Starts) | medium | the two start angles are inverted. Elite track/kick starts leave the block with the centre-of-mass velocity vector close to horizontal (roughly −5° to +10°; Vantorre, Chollet & Seifert 2014; Tor, Pease & Ball 2015) because the block is 0.5–0.75 m above the water — 35–45° is the projectile-range optimum for a launch at water level and would produce a high, slow dive. The ENTRY angle is the steep one: elite entries are about 30–45° to the surface, not 15–20° (a 15–20° entry is a flat "belly" dive that costs speed). | Starts card: take-off 'near horizontal — about 0–10° above the horizontal; the 0.5–0.75 m block height gives the flight'; entry 'about 30–40° to the surface — hands, head, hips and feet through one hole'; the paragraph's 'Takeoff angle: 35–45°' updated to match. Shipped with the copy lens's SI spacing ("0.5–0.75 m", "10–15 m"). | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-03 | technique (Backstroke) vs injury (Neck) | medium | the technique page's backstroke body-position card tells the swimmer to look "toward feet", while the Injury › Neck tab lists "chronic chin-tuck looking at the feet" as a cause of neck pain and says "keep the neck neutral". The two pages contradict each other, and the technique cue is the one that is wrong: the head is held still with the eyes up (toward the ceiling/flags), chin neutral; tucking the chin to look at the feet lifts the head, drops the hips and loads the neck. | Backstroke body-position card: 'eyes up toward the ceiling with the chin neutral (look at the flags, not at your feet — a chin-tuck lifts the head, drops the hips and loads the neck)', now consistent with Injury › Neck. | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-04 | visualizer | medium | (a) R1-DOMAIN-09 established that a steady-state stroke must average net ≈ 0 N over the cycle and fixed freestyle only. The dolphin kick still averages **+50 N** (thrust exceeds drag in 4 of 5 phases: −24, +80, +108, +51, +26 weighted by duration) while its velocity ends the cycle where it started; butterfly averages +24 N, backstroke +22 N, freestyle +19 N (breaststroke +6 N is fine). (b) The velocity trace contradicts the sign of the net force at several keyframe transitions: breaststroke "Streamline Glide" (net −14 N) → "Glide & Streamline" (net −12 N) SPEEDS UP 1.72 → 1.80 m/s in a passive glide; butterfly "Recovery" (−18 N) → "Entry" (−10 N) → "Press" rises 1.66 → 1.70 → 1.72; dolphin "Upbeat Drive" (+51 N) → "Upbeat Peak" (+26 N) → "Heels Up" falls 2.15 → 2.10 → 2.05. The panel is labelled "illustrative", but the one lesson it exists to teach (you decelerate in the glide) is inverted for breaststroke. | viz-human.js STROKE_PATCHES re-keyed so every phase's velocity moves in the direction of its keyframe net force and the duration-weighted net is near zero: breaststroke +2.4 N (glide decays 1.86 → 1.68 → 1.50), butterfly +3.4 N (Recovery 1.70 → Entry 1.62 → Press 1.70 → Catch 1.66 → Keyhole 1.84 → Push 2.04 → Exit 2.10), dolphin +3.8 N (2.10 → 2.00 → 2.14 → 2.22 → 2.24, drag 48/56/70/54/56, thrust 12/100/104/58/12). The mannequin fallback records in index.html were re-keyed the same way (breast +3.5, fly -2.3, dolphin +2.0 N; sign check passes on all five). Verified live: 0 sign mismatches in all strokes after applyPatches. | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-05 | workouts | medium | the prompt tells the model "Volume targets for this level: 45min=…, 60min=…, 75min=…, 90min=…" but the Session Length select also offers **120 min** ("2 hours", value 120) — for that choice no target exists and the model is left to guess. The same target string is sent regardless of Meso Phase and Focus, so "Taper / Pre-Meet" or "Recovery / Easy" sessions are still asked for full base volume (senior 60 min = 3,500–4,500 m), contradicting the Taper page (volume down 30–70 %) that the site says its prompts follow. | VOL_TARGETS is now a per-length table with 120 min for every level (age-group/masters 5000–7000, senior 6000–8500, elite 7000–10000); taper meso scales the target ×0.5–0.6 with 'total volume cut 40-60% from base — keep the race-pace quality short and sharp with full rest', Recovery/Easy focus ×0.7, Speed focus 'keep total volume at the low end'. Verified with a stubbed session/fetch (no live call): 120-min senior taper → '3000-5100m … (taper: …)'; 120-min elite recovery → '4900-7000m'. | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-06 | technique (Underwater) + visualizer (AI concept) | medium | the site's own research note in viz-human.js (dolphin patch `sources`) cites elite toe-to-toe amplitude **0.45 ± 0.06 m** (PMC7739797) and "toe amplitude 0.45–0.6 m" for butterfly; the Technique page and the "Ask the Visualizer" prompt say 25–35 cm — roughly half. The prompt propagates the wrong number into every AI analysis of the dolphin kick. | Technique › Underwater key-point now 'Kick amplitude: About 45–55cm toe-to-toe at the feet (the hips move only ~15cm). Hip-driven.'; VIZ_CONCEPTS.dolphin-kick prompt says '~0.45-0.55m toe-to-toe at the feet, hips ~0.15m' and adds the ~1.9 Hz elite mean rate. Shipped with the copy lens's SI spacing ("45–55 cm", "~15 cm"). | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-07 | nutrition (Daily Fueling) | medium | the carbohydrate ladder puts taper weeks (7–8 g/kg) ABOVE base training (6–7 g/kg) although taper volume drops 30–70 %; energy and carbohydrate needs fall with the volume until the 1–2 day pre-race load (which the Race Day tab already covers at 8–10 g/kg). As written, a swimmer eating 7–8 g/kg through a two-week taper is being told to over-fuel. | Carbohydrate ladder: 'Taper weeks: 5–6 g/kg as volume drops (weight stays stable) — rising to 8–10 g/kg only on the 1–2 days before racing'. | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-08 | race (Pacing Science) | low | the planner's own PATTERNS give 200 FLY halves 47.7 / 52.3 % (second 100 **9.6 %** slower — three times the freestyle fade of 2.8 %) and 200 BR 48.6 / 51.4 % (5.8 %). "A little more" understates a fade of ~10 s in a 1:50 fly. | Pacing Science: 'butterfly and breaststroke 200s fade more — the second 100 is typically 5–6% slower in breaststroke and 8–10% slower in butterfly, so holding the third 50 is a stretch target there' (matches PATTERNS 200 BR 48.6/51.4 and 200 FLY 47.7/52.3). Shipped with the copy lens's "100 m / 200 m / 400 m" spacing and US "minimize". | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-09 | race (Warm-Up) | low | the protocol adds up to 400–600 + 200 + 200 + 100 + 30 + 200 = **1,130–1,330 m**; the box still overstates by ~70 m at both ends (R1-COPY-13 narrowed it from 1,200–1,800 but did not make it add up). | Warm-up box now 'Total: about 1,150–1,350m' = 400–600 + 200 + 200 + 100 + 30 + 200. Shipped as "about 1,150–1,350 m" (copy lens spacing). | fixed |
| R2-DOMAIN-10 | technique (Underwater) | low | the merged R1 sentence contradicts itself: "About 2.0–2.5 Hz (120–150 kicks/min) at full race speed; elite means sit near 1.9 Hz." If the elite mean at maximal effort is 1.9 Hz (the cited PMC7739797 figure), 2.0–2.5 Hz is not the typical full-race-speed rate but the top of the distribution. | Elite rate key-point: 'About 1.9 Hz on average at race speed (≈115 kicks/min), with the fastest sprinters at 2.0–2.5 Hz (120–150). **Round 2 totals:** 120 findings — 120 fixed, 0 partial, 0 deferred, 0 not reproduced. Post-merge fix commits: "forensic r2: post-merge fix" (single Enter-to-submit handler) and "forensic r2: post-merge fix (viz-human.js notes string)".

## Round 3 — 2026-09-18

Round focus: third full sweep of 3.1.1 after the round-2 merge (ca4acab), with each lens first re-checking every round-1/2 fix for regressions and then concentrating on phones (375/390 px), the drawer, tablists and dialogs, and the failure paths — offline, a blocked CDN (Three.js, supabase-js), an unreachable backend, stalled AI streams; the calculators, log, planner, timers and analytics driven with concrete inputs; the 3D lab measured per stroke; the service worker, CSP, manifest, icons and the legal pages; the swimming facts re-verified. Fixes were made on six branches (forensic/r3-copy 26, -visual 22, -function 20, -a11y 21, -code 16, -domain 16) and merged into main one by one; 78 conflict hunks (visual 10 + privacy.html 1, function 4, a11y 12, code 25 + tools/make_logo.py 1, domain 6) were resolved by hand keeping every lens's intent — the cross-references are in the Fix column. Post-merge: check_index.py PASS, dupcheck against HEAD~6 (no new duplicate top-level names; 24 new names, function's authUnavailableNotice and a11y's authUnavailableMsg dropped as superseded with no remaining references), node --check viz-human.js and sw.js, version strings untouched (sw.js VERSION = UPDATES[0].version = footer = 3.1.1). Browser smoke of the merged site (own tab, served copy on :8655): all 22 pages open with no console errors beyond the pane's known service-worker registration failure; the 3D lab builds and all five strokes switch and render (51 draw calls, 75k triangles), arrow keys step and announce the phase, the zoom buttons dolly, wheel zoom is gated on focus/Ctrl, the fallback → Retry / "online" path rebuilds the lab; the drawer opens and closes with focus returned to the burger, ⌘K palette opens/closes, keyboard hints hide under hover:none, Sets tables stack at 375 px with no horizontal scroll; log delete/undo keeps focus in the list and escapes notes; parseTime accepts 1.52.30 / 1:52.30 / 62.30 and rejects 1.52 / 1:75; every logged-out AI entry point explains itself in the page (library present → overlay + message; library blocked → in-page notice only, nav Log In marked, overlay explains with disabled buttons, Enter-key path guarded); privacy/terms/disclaimer/404 carry the CSP and the privacy table has scoped headers; sw.js serves the lie-fi timeout build and the manifest lists the narrow screenshot. Two post-merge fix commits ("forensic r3: post-merge fix"): the first adds Enter-key guards in the four auth actions when supabase-js never loaded and makes vizLabRetry re-attach an already-built lab's canvas; the second restores the space before <br> in the reworded login heading.

### Function & data (function) — 20 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R3-FUNCTION-01 | ai (also the edge function supabase/functions/ask-ai/index.ts, read-only) | high | AI Coach wedges itself after the 10th exchange: the 11th request starts with an assistant turn | sendToAI: after slice(-20) the history sheds any leading assistant turn (while(history[0].role!=='user') shift()). Verified with stubbed fetch: sends 10-13 all start with a user turn (19 entries) instead of assistant. Edge function (read-only) still slices -20 unguarded; recommend the same shift there. | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-02 | profile (My PRs), calculator (Race Splits, Pace Zones, Time Converter), planner (Goal Times, Race Predictor, Split Planner), profile › Import Backup | medium | parseTime rejects every bare-seconds time from 60.00 to 99.99, so "62.30" is "invalid" in My PRs, the calculators and the planner (but valid in Analytics) | parseTime regex widened to (\d{1,4}(?:\.\d{1,3})?) for the seconds group; secs>=60 rejected only with a minutes prefix; 10 s floor and <3600 cap kept. Planner parseT fallback regex mirrored. Verified: '65'->65, '62.30'->62.3, '99.99'->99.99, '1:75'->null; My PRs 62.30 stores 1:02.30, Pace Zones '65' renders 6 zones, Race Predictor accepts 62.30. Merger: Merged with R3-CODE-01: parseTime first rewrites the keypad form "1.52.30" → "1:52.30", then applies the widened bare-seconds regex. | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-03 | log (data via profile › Import Backup) | medium | Deleting one Training Log session deletes every session that shares its id; imported backups are never de-duplicated | readLog repair pass and sanitizeLogEntries keep a seen Set and re-id duplicates; deleteLogEntry now removes exactly one entry (no-op if the id is unknown) and offers a 6 s Undo button in #log-notice (undoLogDelete restores it). Verified: three id:5 sessions import as three unique ids; one tap deletes one; Undo restores it. Merger: Merged with R3-A11Y-07: deleteLogEntry keeps the Undo notice and moves focus to the delete button at the same index; undoLogDelete focuses the restored session's delete button. | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-04 | visualizer | medium | 3D lab arrow-key phase stepping collapses key presses that land in the same frame (holding the key steps one phase per frame at most) | New phaseIdxFromClock3D() walks the phase durations from clockT synchronously; the arrow-key handler steps from it (jumpToPhase3D pauses itself). Verified: three ArrowLefts inside one frame from Phase 4 land on Phase 1 (was Phase 3); wrap-around from Phase 1 to Phase 6 works. | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-05 | timers (Pace Clock › Send-off calculator) | low | Send-off calculator keeps the previous 100-row table on screen when a new run errors | soFail(msg) sets the status, hides #tlt-so-wrap and #tlt-so-copy and empties body/summary/text; used on all three early returns. Verified: 25 m 5 s, 50 m 12 s and 25 yd 7.5 s rejected; 25 m 17.8 s still scores 35.8. | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-07 | timers (Tempo Trainer) | low | Tempo trainer status line goes stale when the tempo is nudged while running | tpSet refreshes #tlt-tempo-status via new tpRunningMsg() when the tempo changes while running (nudge, slider, arrow keys); accent change does the same; tpStart reuses tpRunningMsg. Verified: '3-2-1, then 73 strokes/min.' -> nudge -> '78 strokes/min.' -> slider -> '81 strokes/min.' | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-08 | profile (My PRs › Import Backup) | low | Import Backup says it "replaces" the PRs on the device but actually merges: PRs missing from the file survive | importBackup clears every valid pr_* key before writing the file's PRs when fileHasPR, so the confirm's 'replaces' is true. | R3-FUNCTION-10 | profile (My PRs), planner (Goal Times), calculator | low | parseTime's "no swim time is under 10 s" rule is bypassed with a minutes prefix ("0:05" is stored as a 5-second PR) | parseTime applies the 10 s floor to every form (if(v<10) return null). Verified: '0:05' and '0:09.99' -> null; '10' -> 10. | fixed |
| R3-FUNCTION-11 | ai (also workouts) | low | AI Coach discards a partially streamed answer when the stream breaks mid-way | fullText hoisted above the try in sendToAI and _generateWorkoutInner; the catch keeps the partial text (bubble gets a 'Connection interrupted…' trailer, the assistant turn is pushed) and only rolls back the user turn when nothing arrived; the generator formats the partial sets and appends a red note, actions shown. Verified with a stream that errors after one chunk. Merger: Merged with R3-CODE-03: a stream that was stopped, stalled (idle timeout) or broken after tokens arrived keeps the partial answer (bubble trailer says which); with nothing received the user turn is rolled back, the timeout/stop/network message shown and the question put back in the box. | fixed |

### Accessibility (a11y) — 21 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R3-A11Y-01 | all (⌘K palette) | high | Palette: Enter on "Close search" (or the "/" toggle) opens the first result instead of closing | onDialogKey: the Enter branch now returns unless focus is on #tl-pal-input, so the ✕ Close button and the '/' checkbox keep native Enter/Space activation. Verified: Enter on Close no longer navigates (defaultPrevented false, hash unchanged) and native click closes; Enter in the input still opens the highlighted result. | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-02 | technique, videos, sets, visualizer, calculator, nutrition, race, taper, mental, injury, timers, planner, analytics (13 tablists); also the AI mode chips on phones | medium | Tab-strip focus ring is clipped to two thin side bars on every tabbed page, at every width | Added .tab-btn:focus-visible and .ai-mode-btn:focus-visible to the existing inset-ring rule (outline-offset:-3px), so the ring is drawn inside the button instead of being clipped by the overflow-x:auto strip. Verified the rule is live (strip h == button h, overflow auto reproduced first). | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-03 | visualizer (offline / CDN blocked / SRI mismatch) | medium | When the Three.js CDN is unreachable the 3D lab keeps `role="application"` + arrow-key promise and 21 live-looking dead controls | New vizLabUnavailable(msg, retry) / vizLabRetry() used by both the CDN onerror and the WebGL-unsupported branch: removes role/tabindex/aria-label from the empty #viz3d-container, renders the reason as text, tlAnnounce()s it, disables (disabled + aria-disabled) the 20 render-only .viz-btn controls (AI Reconstruct excluded), and adds a real 'Retry loading the 3D lab' button. Retry restores the attributes, re-enables the controls, refocuses the container and re-runs ensureViz3DReady(). Verified stubbed-CDN failure -> role null, 20 disabled, announced; Retry with CDN restored -> THREE loaded, canvas built, 0 disabled, role=application back. Merger: Merged with R3-COPY-05 and R3-CODE-02 (vizLabUnavailable gained an optional html argument; vizLabRetry also re-attaches an already-built lab's canvas — post-merge fix). | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-04 | all (nav Log In), ai, workouts, visualizer (Ask the Visualizer) | medium | Offline / blocked supabase-js: "Log In" and AI "Send" fall back to a blocking `alert()` | openAuthGate returns true/false; the failed branch calls markLoginUnavailable() (aria-disabled + title 'Sign-in service unavailable' / 'You are offline') and tlAnnounce(authUnavailableMsg()) instead of alert(). sendToAI shows the message as an AI bubble, generateWorkout via a new #gen-notice (role=alert) and tlNotice, generateVisualization via #viz-notice, generateMotion via #motion-status. onSupabaseScript('failed') and the early __sbScriptFailed path mark the Log In button at load. Verified all four entry points with supabase stubbed out: zero alerts, inline notices present, nav button annotated. Merger: Merged with R3-FUNCTION-14 / R3-CODE-13: markLoginUnavailable() kept (aria-disabled + title on the nav button, at load and on open); the inline notices come from function's branch; openAuthGate returns true/false. | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-05 | ai | medium | AI conversation log is a vertical scroll region with no keyboard access | #ai-messages gets tabindex=0 and aria-describedby=ai-messages-hint (sr-only 'Scrollable conversation. When focused, use the arrow keys to scroll it.'); added .ai-messages:focus-visible to the inset-ring rule because .ai-wrapper is overflow:hidden. Verified attributes at runtime. Merger: Selector shipped as .ai-messages[tabindex]:focus-visible so the inset ring outranks the generic [tabindex] rule. | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-06 | all 22 routes | medium | Route change moves focus to an unnamed `<div>`; screen readers get no page-change announcement | activatePage sets role=region + aria-label (short PAGE_TITLES name, 'Home' for home) on the page div before focusing it, and tlAnnounce()s '<title> page' on every non-initial navigation. Verified on all 22 routes (e.g. DIV#page-zones role=region label 'Swim Training Zones', live region 'Swim Training Zones page'). | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-07 | log | medium | Deleting a log session drops keyboard focus to `<body>` | deleteLogEntry remembers the deleted row's index and, after renderLog(), focusLogAfterChange() focuses the delete button now at that index (or the last one), falling back to the SESSION HISTORY heading (new id log-history-heading, tabindex=-1) when the list is empty; clearLog does the same. Verified: middle delete -> next row's delete button, last delete -> previous, only delete -> heading (was BODY). Merger: Merged with R3-FUNCTION-03 (focus management added to the undo-able delete; undo also restores focus). | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-08 | all (update toast after a service-worker activate) | low | Update toast: Escape does not dismiss it, dismissing it strands focus, and the ✕ glyph is inside the live region | Toast is now a role=region 'Update available' inserted right after <nav> (before <main>) with role=status/aria-live on the message span only; ✕ glyph aria-hidden; a document keydown handler dismisses on Escape (deferring to an open palette/auth dialog/drawer unless focus is in the toast); hideUpdateToast restores focus to the element focused before the toast appeared (else #nav-updates / main). Verified placement (NAV -> toast -> MAIN), Escape dismisses and returns focus to nav-search-btn, ✕ click likewise, palette Escape untouched. | fixed |
| R3-A11Y-09 | visualizer | low | 3D lab: stepping phases with the arrow keys announces nothing | The #viz3d-container arrow-key handler now calls tlAnnounce('Phase N of M: <name>') after jumpToPhase3D. | R3-CODE-01 | profile (My PRs), planner (Goal Times, Race Predictor, Fit exponent, Split Planner), analytics (Stroke rate, CSS) | high | `inputmode="decimal"` on every m:ss time field: phone keypads have no ":" key, so minutes cannot be typed on My PRs, the Race Planner and Analytics | Reproduced (45 inputmode=decimal fields incl. the 36 templated PR/goal fields; parseTime('152.30') and parseTime('1.52.30') both null). Removed inputmode="decimal" from the nine m:ss fields (#pl-pd-time, #pl-fit-ta, #pl-fit-tb, #pl-sp-goal, #an-sr-time, #an-css-400, #an-css-200, the pr-in-* template, the pl-goal-* template; kept on #an-sw-time and #an-sr-dist), parseTime now rewrites the number-keypad form "1.52.30" to "1:52.30" (a single-dot "1.52" stays a rejected typo), and #pr-format-hint / the PR error hint say so. Verified: only an-sw-time/an-sr-dist keep decimal; parseTime('1.52.30')=112.3; a PR typed as 1.52.30 saves and normalises to 1:52.30; the planner predictor accepts it. Merger: Merged with R3-FUNCTION-02 (keypad rewrite + bare seconds ≥ 60 both accepted). | fixed |
| R3-CODE-02 | visualizer | medium | 3D lab: after a failed Three.js load the retry renders the scene underneath the "could not load" message (canvas fully clipped), and a failed viz-human.js load is never retried | Reproduced (fallback markup + resizeViz3D -> canvas offsetTop 380, visible height 0). initViz3D removes any .viz-fallback before appending the canvas; loadHumanScripts' onerror drains the queue then resets humanScriptState to 'idle' and removes the dead <script>; new retryHumanModules() (called from ensureViz3DReady when the lab is already built) re-injects viz-human.js and swaps the mannequin for the human (lighting, stroke patches, phase bar); a window 'online' listener re-runs ensureViz3DReady while the lab is active. Verified in the browser with a rewritten script src: first Three.js load 404 -> fallback; revisit -> fallback gone, canvas at top 0; first viz-human.js load 404 -> state 'idle' + mannequin; revisit -> viz-human.js loaded, swimmer.isHuman true, '✓ Male swimmer ready'. Merger: Merged with R3-A11Y-03: the "online" listener calls vizLabRetry() when the fallback is up (restores role/tabindex/label), else ensureViz3DReady(). | fixed |
| R3-CODE-03 | ai, workouts, visualizer (AI analysis + motion director) | medium | AI streams have no timeout, no AbortController and no Stop control: a stalled request leaves the chat/generator/analysis wedged until reload and discards the typed question | Reproduced (never-settling fetch: Send disabled, input cleared, no stop control). Added aiStreamGuard() (AbortController + 20 s idle timer reset per chunk) and wired its signal into all four fetch sites (coach chat, workout generator, visualizer analysis, motion director) with guard.touch() per chunk; timeouts report 'The coach did not answer in time — try again.'; the chat has a STOP button beside SEND (visible only while streaming, same size, ghost style) via stopAI()/setAIStreaming(); on timeout/stop/network failure the user turn is rolled back and the question is put back into the input; a stop mid-answer keeps the partial reply. Verified: Stop -> button re-enabled, 'Stopped.', input restored; stalled fetch -> timeout message at 21.5 s with input restored; generator with one chunk then a stall -> timeout message, button re-enabled. Merger: Merged with R3-FUNCTION-11 (partial text kept on timeout as well as on Stop; AI_NETWORK_MSG used for the no-text failure); generateMotion distinguishes timeout, no-connection (R3-COPY-02) and unusable-reply. | fixed |
| R3-CODE-04 | PWA / every navigation | medium | sw.js network-first has no timeout: on a flaky ("lie-fi") connection the cached shell is never used until the request itself times out | Reproduced with the auditor's harness against the worktree sw.js (lie-fi navigation served after 6003 ms). networkFirst(event, req) now races the network against a 3 s timer when a cached copy exists, serves the cached shell on timeout and keeps the network promise alive with event.waitUntil so the late answer still refreshes the cache; nothing changes when nothing is cached. Harness: fast network 201 ms fresh; lie-fi 3002 ms cached-shell (waitUntil called once); offline 1 ms cached-shell; uncached page on a slow network still waits for the network (5004 ms fresh). node --check passes; VERSION untouched. | fixed |
| R3-CODE-05 | visualizer | medium | 3D lab: the wheel handler always calls preventDefault, so the page cannot be scrolled while the pointer is over the canvas (39–54 % of the viewport) | Reproduced (WheelEvent over the unfocused canvas: defaultPrevented true). onWheel now returns without preventDefault unless the lab has focus (#viz3d-container, the canvas or a descendant) or Ctrl/⌘ is held (trackpad pinch); onPointerDown focuses the container (preventScroll) so a click enables wheel zoom; help text now reads 'Click the lab, then scroll to zoom (or hold Ctrl/⌘)' and the aria-label mentions it. Verified: unfocused wheel not prevented, ctrl+wheel prevented, click focuses the container, focused wheel prevented. Merger: Merged with R3-VISUAL-19 / R3-A11Y-19 (hint and aria-label wording combined). | fixed |
| R3-CODE-06 | auth overlay (every page) | medium | Sign-in, sign-up and password reset show the browser's raw "Failed to fetch" when Supabase is unreachable; sign-up ignores the availability RPC's error | Reproduced with fetch rejecting: login/forgot showed 'Failed to fetch'; rpc('username_taken') returned error 'TypeError: Failed to fetch'. Added authErrMsg(err) (status 0 / AuthRetryableFetchError / 'Failed to fetch'-style messages / navigator.onLine false -> 'Cannot reach the sign-in service — check your connection and try again.') at the login, signup, forgot and new-password sites; doSignup now handles taken.error (shows the message, re-enables the button, returns before signUp). Verified: login, forgot and sign-up all show the readable sentence with the button re-enabled. Merger: Merged into authErrorText() (see R3-COPY-01). | fixed |
