### Swimming domain accuracy (domain) — 16 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R3-DOMAIN-01 | taper + planner (Meet Countdown) | medium | the site gives sprinters the SHORT taper (7–14 days) and distance swimmers the LONG one (14–21 days, volume down 60–70 % in the last week). Coaching practice and the taper literature are the other way round: sprinters carry more muscle and need the longer taper (2–3 weeks, up to 4+ in some programmes), while distance swimmers lose aerobic feel quickly and taper for 1–2 weeks with only a modest volume drop (Mujika… | Reproduced live (taperWindow(16,'sprint') = 'Pre-taper', taperWindow(5,'distance') = 'volume down 60–70%'). Swapped and re-authored both Taper-page blocks (SPRINT 14–21 days: wk3 −20–30% speed/power kept, wk2 −40–50% race-pace/power touches, wk1 −60–70% short fast work; DISTANCE 7–14 days: wk2 −20–30% one solid aerobic set, wk1 −40–50% aerobic rhythm + race-pace 100s/200s, day before 30–40 min easy with builds) plus a one-line 'Why sprinters taper longer' rationale box; planner select options, pl-fine caption, TAPER_LABEL, the taperWindow comment and both branches (sprint >21 / 21–14 / 14–7 / ≤7; distance >14 / 14–7 / ≤7), CHECKPOINTS d:21 ('For a sprint taper… Distance swimmers keep training fully for another week'), d:14 (both tapers named), d:7 (sprint 60–70% / distance 40–50%) and d:1 (distance day-before variant). Verified: sprint 25/16/10/5 d → Pre-taper / wk3 −20–30% / wk2 −40–50% / wk1 −60–70%; distance 16/10/5 → Pre-taper / wk2 −20–30% / wk1 −40–50%; countdown card and checkpoints render the new text. | fixed |
| R3-DOMAIN-02 | visualizer (3D lab, freestyle + backstroke) | medium | freestyle and backstroke are the only strokes without a `rate` multiplier, so at "1× Normal" they cycle at 0.55 Hz = **33 cycles/min** (one arm cycle every 1.8 s — a drill / warm-up tempo) while the panel reads **1.80–1.94 m/s** (mean 1.86 m/s = 53.9 s per 100 m, i.e. 100 m race pace). That is 1.856 / 0.55 = **3.37 m per cycle** for freestyle and 1.78 / 0.55 = **3.24 m** for backstroke — far outside the elite… | Reproduced live (freestyle 33 cpm / 1.856 m/s = 3.37 m per cycle; backstroke 33 / 1.781 / 3.24). Added rate: 1.45 to the freestyle patch and rate: 1.40 to the backstroke patch in viz-human.js STROKE_PATCHES (numbers only, no pose change) and changed the freestyle phase-0 caption to '(the model shows a compact ~22° whole-body roll)'. Verified after: freestyle 47.9 cpm → 2.33 m/cycle, backstroke 46.2 cpm → 2.31 m/cycle; lab drives and renders with no console errors. The proposed What's New 3.1.2 line and sw.js VERSION bump were NOT made (fix rules reserve UPDATES/VERSION for the release step). | fixed |
| R3-DOMAIN-03 | technique (Freestyle › Body rotation) | medium | the sprint/distance roll magnitudes are inverted. Body roll DEcreases as swimming speed and stroke rate increase: sprinters swim flatter and faster-turning, distance swimmers roll more (Psycharakis & Sanders 2010 review: "faster swimmers roll their shoulders less"; Yanai 2003: roll amplitude falls with stroke frequency; Cappaert et al. 1995 Olympic 100-m data). Elite shoulder roll is about ±45–60° at 200/400 pace… | Technique › Body rotation now reads 'Shoulder roll: about 45–60° each side at distance pace, less (30–40°) in the 50/100 where turnover is higher; the hips roll roughly half as much.'; VIZ_CONCEPTS['freestyle-rotation'] appends '(roll is smaller at sprint speed, larger at distance pace)'. Verified in the DOM. | fixed |
| R3-DOMAIN-04 | visualizer (dolphin kick) | low | the dolphin runs at 96 kicks/min (1.6 Hz — the patch's own comment calls this "the slow half of real", 70–80 % speed per its PMC7739797 source) but the panel shows 2.10–2.24 m/s (mean 2.13 m/s = 47 s per 100 m, faster than the 100 free world record). 2.13 / 1.6 = **1.33 m per kick**; elite underwater kicking is about 2.0 m/s at 1.9 Hz ≈ 1.05 m per kick, and at 70–80 % rate the speed is ~1.5–1.8 m/s. R1-DOMAIN-10… | Reproduced live (dolphin 95.7 kicks/min, mean 2.128 m/s = 1.33 m per kick). Scaled the five dolphin patch velocities to the rig tempo keeping the R2 sign pattern: 1.68 / 1.60 / 1.72 / 1.78 / 1.80 (duration-weighted mean 1.706 m/s = 1.07 m per kick), with the rate comment updated. Verified live: mPerKick 1.07, panel reads 1.67 m/s in Heels Up. Tempo (96 kicks/min) and What's New line untouched. | fixed |
| R3-DOMAIN-05 | calculator (Golf Score) + technique (Golf Score system) | low | neither the calculator nor the technique card says whether "stroke count" is arm strokes (each hand entry) or cycles. The Analytics page is explicit ("Freestyle and backstroke: count every hand entry (each arm)") and its SWOLF note says the Golf Score is "the same idea", but a swimmer who counts cycles gets a score ~15–20 lower and a flattering verdict: 32 s + 14 cycles → **46 "World-class efficiency"**… | Reproduced (32 s + 14 → 46 'World-class efficiency'). Calculator label is now 'Stroke Count (per 50 m, each arm — count every hand entry)', the SCORE GUIDE box adds 'Free/back: count each arm; fly/breast: one stroke per cycle — the same convention as the Analytics SWOLF calculator', and the technique card formula adds '(count each arm in free and back; one stroke per cycle in fly and breast)'. Verified in the DOM. Merger: Merged with R3-CODE-14 (enterkeyhint kept on the golf inputs) and R3-COPY-21 ("Split (seconds)"). | fixed |
| R3-DOMAIN-06 | zones (Zone 4–5 card) + calculator (pace zones) | low | the Zone 5 definition contradicts itself physiologically. 10–30 s all-out reps with full rest are alactic / neural speed work: blood lactate stays modest and heart rate does not reach max, precisely because the rest is full. 8+ mmol/L and max HR describe lactate-production work (50–100 m maximal efforts on short rest, or race efforts), not 10–30 s sprints. The Sets page ("Speed work is Zone 4–5 … 10×25 sprint,… | Zone 4–5 card: 'Zone 5: sprint / anaerobic — two kinds. Alactic speed: 10–30 s efforts on full rest; lactate stays low and HR is not the guide. Lactate production: 50–100 m maximal efforts (8.0+ mmol/L, HR near max), 2–5 min rest, 1–2× a week at most.'; zone bar Z5 row '8.0+ mmol on lactate work · rest-dependent'; calcPaceZones Zone 5 desc 'Speed & lactate work · rest-dependent'. Verified live (calculator from 1:00 prints the new desc). Merger: Merged with R3-COPY-08/17 ("48–72 h", "VO₂ max" casing kept). | fixed |
| R3-DOMAIN-07 | technique (Butterfly › Undulation) + visualizer (AI concept) | low | three site figures for how much the hips move disagree: the butterfly card and the AI prompt say 20–30 cm, the Underwater card (R2-DOMAIN-06 fix) says "the hips move only ~15 cm", and the 3D butterfly rig moves the hip line **10 cm** peak-to-peak (`bobAmp: 0.05`; measured 10.0 cm over a full cycle on the human rig). The wave grows head → toe (chest ≈ 5 cm, hips ≈ 15, toes 45–60 per the module's sources), so 20–30… | Butterfly card: 'Hip amplitude: about 10–20 cm — the wave grows toward the feet (toes 45–60 cm).'; VIZ_CONCEPTS['butterfly-undulation']: 'hip amplitude (~0.1-0.2 m, growing to 0.45-0.6 m at the toes)'. No rig change. Verified in the DOM. | fixed |

**Round 3 totals:** 123 findings — 121 fixed, 0 partial, 2 deferred, 0 not reproduced. Deferred: R3-COPY-20 and R3-CODE-16 (see their rows). Post-merge fix commits: "forensic r3: post-merge fix" (auth Enter-key guards, vizLabRetry canvas) and "forensic r3: post-merge fix (space before <br> in the LOG IN TO heading)".

## Round 4 — 2026-09-18

Round focus: fourth full sweep of 3.1.1 after the round-3 merge (cd3540e) — each lens first re-checked the round-1–3 fixes for regressions, then looked for what the earlier rounds could not see: cross-page consistency of the same fact (lab vs Technique, home cards vs the pages they describe, Sets vs Dryland vs Injury), the phone at 375 px measured element by element (nav brand, pills, drawer typography, card padding, stat tiles, table headers, footer column), the failure paths that survive a merge (Clear during a stream, double delete, double-submitted auth, future-dated sessions, world-record-breaking split plans, the split taker across a page change), assistive-technology semantics (toggle state beyond colour, inert behind the palette, decorative arrows and emoji, key-point lists, scrollable tables), the published site on GitHub Pages (Jekyll exposing supabase/ and tools/, robots.txt on a project site, manifest id, service-worker 5xx handling, navigation preload, Google Fonts offline), and the swimming physics in the lab (phase labels vs the pose, dolphin thrust vs foot speed, breaststroke thrust vs the kick finish, zone bands, golf-score bands under the per-arm count, an elite split pattern). Fixes were made on six branches (forensic/r4-copy 31, -visual 22, -function 10, -a11y 14, -code 11, -domain 17) and merged into main one by one; 16 conflict hunks (visual 1, function 1, a11y 5, code 2 + README.md 1, domain 4) were resolved by hand keeping every lens's intent — the cross-references are in the Fix column. Post-merge: check_index.py PASS, dupcheck against HEAD~6 (no new duplicate top-level names; 5 new names incl. fmtLogDate, aiServerText, readAIStream, _chatGen, _logUndoTimer), node --check viz-human.js and sw.js, the code lens's service-worker harness reproduces its expected output byte for byte on the merged sw.js, version strings untouched (sw.js VERSION = UPDATES[0].version = footer = 3.1.1). Browser smoke of the merged site (own tab, served copy on :8655, 375 / 768 / 1280): all 22 pages open with no console errors beyond the pane's known service-worker registration failure; the 3D lab builds (58k-triangle human, 75k triangles rendered) and all five strokes switch and render, arrow keys step every phase and the panel labels the nearest keyframe in all five strokes (0/40 mismatches each); the drawer opens and closes with main inert and focus back on the burger, the ⌘K palette inerts main/nav and restores them, no-results and Install actions render; the log's undo stack restores two deletes, the 30-day summary hides a future-only log and Analytics explains it, zone-bar labels fit at 375, the golf bands, elite split pattern, sub-world-record rejection and contiguous pace zones compute, the split taker keeps timing across a page change, the coach chat streams through readAIStream and survives Clear mid-stream, the auth consent row is 20 px / 44 px, static pages have the footer outside main and 404 no longer redirects. One post-merge fix commit ("forensic r4: post-merge fix"): the log heading and delete notice use the formatted date, and the AI welcome is tightened so the R4-DOMAIN-02 disclosure fits the phone pane (R4-VISUAL-03).

### Function & data (function) — 10 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R4-FUNCTION-01 | ai | medium | Clearing the chat while an answer is streaming leaves an orphaned assistant turn, so the next question fails | Reproduced with a mocked SSE stream (history became [assistant] and the next Send posted [assistant,user]). Added a chat generation counter _chatGen: sendToAI captures gen and bails out after every await (getSession, fetch, resp.json, each reader.read, and in the catch) when it is stale; clearChat() now bumps _chatGen, calls stopAI() (aborts the fetch) and setAIStreaming(false) before clearing. Verified: history stays [], the Stop button hides, the next question posts [user] only and streams normally; also verified the non-abort path (stream that ignores the abort) via the post-read guard. Merger (× R4-CODE-11): the code lens replaced the chat's SSE loop with the shared readAIStream; readAIStream gained an optional stale() callback checked after every read (cancels the reader and returns the text so far) and the chat re-checks the generation after the await. Verified with a mocked stream that ignores the abort: history stays [], no stray bubble, the next question posts [user] only. | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-02 | log | medium | Deleting two sessions in a row loses the first one for good: single-slot Undo and focus lands on the next ✕ | Reproduced (delete 3 then 2 → Undo restored only 2; 3 gone). _logUndo is now a stack with one shared timer: each delete pushes, the notice reads 'N sessions deleted — latest <date · type>. Undo all' (single delete unchanged: 'Session deleted — …. Undo'), the status announces the count, Undo restores every pending session ('2 sessions restored.') and focuses the last-deleted session's ✕; the 6 s timer (restarted on each delete) clears the whole stack and the notice. Kept the focus-next-✕ behaviour from R3 since a double delete is now fully recoverable. Merger (× R4-COPY-13): the notice now prints the formatted date (post-merge fix). | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-03 | every page (Log in / Sign up dialog) | medium | Sign-up, log-in and new-password can be submitted twice while the first request is in flight | Reproduced with mocked sb.auth (two calls each for signInWithPassword, signUp, updateUser on a double invoke). Added 'if (btn && btn.disabled) return;' before the disable/label change in doSignup, doLogin and doSetNewPassword, mirroring doForgotPassword. Verified one call each; buttons re-enable and labels restore after the call settles. | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-04 | log, analytics | low | A session dated tomorrow is counted by the Training Log's 30-day summary but Analytics says "Nothing to analyze yet" | Reproduced (tomorrow-dated session → Log 30-day summary showed 1 session while Analytics showed the empty state). renderLog's 30-day window now also requires e.date <= logTodayISO(0) (summary hidden for a future-only log); the analytics empty state gained <p id="an-empty-future"> filled in render(): 'One session is dated in the future — it will appear here once that date arrives.' / 'N sessions are dated …', hidden whenever the body is shown or no future entries exist. Verified all three states. | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-05 | calculator (Race Splits) | low | Race Splits accepts a 100 m in 38.00 and prints an 18.30 s first 50 although its own message says under 19 s/50 is "faster than any 50 m ever swum" | Reproduced (100 m in 38 → 18.30 s / 19.70 s rows, no notice; 200 in 1:16 likewise). The avg<19 pre-check is gone; the splits are built first and rejected when Math.min(splits) < 20.91 with 'The fastest 50 in this plan would be X s — under the 50 m freestyle world record of 20.91 s. Check the distance and time.' Verified: 100/38, 200/1:16 (even and surge) and 100/41.82 rejected; real world-record times (100 in 46.40, 200 in 1:42, 1500 in 14:31) still pass. | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-06 | timers (Tempo Trainer) | low | Tempo trainer: Resume drops the tempo readout from the status line | Reproduced (Resume → 'Running.'). tpResume now sets 'Resumed — '+tpRunningMsg(). Verified: pause, nudge while paused (82→83), resume → 'Resumed — 83 strokes/min.' | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-07 | timers (Interval Timer) | low | Interval timer readouts disagree with each other for half of every second (round vs ceil) | Reproduced (6/2 × 2 at ~1.6 s: 'WORK 5 … Elapsed 0:02 · Remaining 0:12 · Total 0:14'). itRender now uses fmtMS(Math.floor(el)) and fmtMS(Math.ceil(IT.total-el)); itPause's 'Paused at' uses the same floor. Verified: 'WORK 5 … Elapsed 0:01 · Remaining 0:13 · Total 0:14' and at ~2.5 s 'WORK 4 … Elapsed 0:02 · Remaining 0:12'. | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-08 | profile (My PRs › Export Backup) | low | Backup file name uses the UTC date, so an evening export in the Americas is dated tomorrow | Source fix: a.download uses logTodayISO(0) (local date) instead of toISOString().slice(0,10). Verified by intercepting the anchor click: tidelyne-backup-<local date>.json (the pane's clock is America/New_York at an hour where local == UTC, so the mismatch itself is not demonstrable here; the exported field inside the file stays ISO/UTC). | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-09 | router (every page) | low | Hash routes are case-sensitive: `#Zones` or `#TIMERS` silently lands on Home and erases the hash | Reproduced (#Zones → Home, hash erased). activatePage now normalises the route first (split('?')[0].toLowerCase()) when that page exists and is not an auth-callback hash, and writes the canonical hash back with history.replaceState when the address bar held the raw form. Verified: #Zones → page-zones/#zones, #TIMERS → #timers, #technique?x=1 → #technique, nav aria-current follows; unknown hashes still land on Home with the hash cleared, the #main skip anchor still focuses main, and an #error_description=… callback hash is left untouched. | fixed |
| R4-FUNCTION-10 | timers (Pace Clock › Split taker) | low | Leaving the Timers page kills a running split taker (the tempo and interval timers only pause) | Reproduced (start split taker → #zones → 'Stopped at 0:00.82 — Start begins a new one', button START). stopAll() now only stops the split taker's frame loop and wake lock (spLoopStop(); wlDrop('split')) and leaves SP.status='running'; onShow_timers re-holds the wake lock, repaints the time at once via spFrame() (which restarts the loop) and sets the sub-line to 'Running — kept timing while you were away · tap for each split'. Verified: after leaving and returning the main button is still SPLIT and a split taken on return reads the true elapsed clock (0:08.70 after 0:00.50); Stop/Reset unchanged. | fixed |

### Accessibility (a11y) — 14 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R4-A11Y-01 | visualizer (stroke, view, model, skin, speed, Forces, phase bar), ai (mode chips), timers (Strokes/Cycles seg), analytics (Session type/Zone, Distance/Minutes segs) | medium | Pressed/selected state of every toggle group is conveyed by colour alone (1.4.1 / 1.4.11) | Reproduced (computed styles: on/off differed only in colour, no shadow/weight change). CSS only: .viz-btn.active, .ai-mode-btn.active and the timers seg get border-color:var(--cyan)+inset 1px cyan ring (a solid 2px ring, ~11:1 vs the 0.07-alpha rest border); the analytics seg (border:none) gets an inset 2px ring; .phase-step.active gets the tabs' inset 3px cyan underline bar. Bold was avoided so widths do not jump when the selection moves. Verified: active viz-btn border rgb(0,212,255) + inset shadow, off unchanged; timers/analytics segs and phase step likewise; hover rule (:not(.active)) stays distinct. Merger (× R4-VISUAL-12): analytics pressed ring plus the visual lens's .an-table-btn min-height. | fixed |
| R4-A11Y-02 | all (palette) | medium | The command palette (⌘K) leaves the page behind it reachable — `aria-modal` only, no `inert` (unlike the auth dialog and the drawer) | Reproduced (main.inert===false with the palette open). Root cause found on the way: #tl-palette was a child of <main>, so inerting main literally would have inerted the palette itself. Moved the 22-line palette block (position:fixed, visually a no-op) to just after </main>, and added setPalInert(on) in the palette IIFE (same data-tl-inert marker pattern as setAuthInert, marker 'pal') called after lockScroll() in openPalette and after unlockScroll() (before focus return) in closePalette. Verified: palette parent is BODY; open -> main/nav inert with marker 'pal', palette not inert, input focused; close -> un-inerted, marker cleared, focus returns to #nav-search-btn even though nav had been inert. | fixed |
| R4-A11Y-03 | planner (Goal Times, 18 inputs) | medium | Planner goal-time fields have no visible keyboard focus ring — the id-scoped `outline:none` beats the site-wide `:focus-visible` rule | Reproduced by cascade (#page-planner .pl-goal-input{outline:none} at (1,1,0) beats input:focus-visible at (0,1,1)). Added #page-planner .pl-goal-input:focus-visible{outline:2px solid var(--cyan);outline-offset:2px} right after the :focus rule; confirmed in the CSSOM. (Pointer/keyboard focus could not be exercised live: the hidden Browser pane reports document.hasFocus()===false, so :focus pseudo-classes never match there.) | fixed |
| R4-A11Y-04 | log | low | Training-log validation errors are not associated with the field (aria-describedby missing) — `fail()` re-implements half of `tlNotice` | Reproduced from source. saveLogEntry's fail() now calls tlNotice('log-notice',msg,null,id) and only focuses; the manual aria-invalid loop was dropped because the initial tlNotice('log-notice','') already clears the previous field. Verified live: future date -> #log-date aria-invalid=true + aria-describedby=log-notice, focus on it; then bad distance -> #log-date cleared, #log-distance flagged; successful save clears the flag. | fixed |
| R4-A11Y-05 | log (Session History) | low | Session-history entries all get the same heading ("Swim" / "Dryland" / "AM Pool"); the date sits outside the heading | Reproduced from source. <h3 class="log-title"> now appends <span class="sr-only"> — {date}</span> (escaped). Verified: two seeded sessions render headings 'AM Pool — 2026-09-11' and 'AM Pool — 2026-09-10'; visible layout unchanged (sr-only). Merger (× R4-COPY-13): the sr-only date is the formatted one (post-merge fix). | fixed |
| R4-A11Y-06 | home, profile, technique, visualizer | low | Decorative emoji still exposed to screen readers: home card icons ×6, "Streaming AI ✦", profile avatar, technique 🎯 cue boxes ×9, 3D-lab 🔬 and "✓" | Reproduced (🔬 span exposed, home card icons plain text). aria-hidden="true" on the six .card-icon spans, the profile avatar div and both 🔬 .icon spans (static + the imggen innerHTML placeholder); 'Streaming AI ✦' wraps the ✦; the 8 cue boxes wrap '🎯 ' in <span aria-hidden="true">; the '✓ ' prefix was dropped from the two humanStatus strings and the 'Reconstructed:' default. Verified with a text-node scan over every .page: 0 emoji/dingbat text nodes outside aria-hidden; human-status now reads 'Male swimmer ready — 58k triangles.' Merger (× R4-COPY-21): aria-hidden icons kept with the copy lens's new card eyebrows. | fixed |
| R4-A11Y-07 | calculator (4), planner (Predict →, Fit exponent →, Generate splits →, 18× Plan splits →), analytics (3), timers (Leave times →), profile (Calculate Pace Zones →), season (4 "View official … →"), auth dialog (LOG IN →, CREATE ACCOUNT →, SEND RESET LINK →, SET NEW PASSWORD →), privacy/terms/disclaimer ("← Back to Tidelyne"), 404 ("Go to Tidelyne →") | low | Trailing "→" / leading "←" glyphs are part of 20+ control names ("Calculate →", "LOG IN →", "Plan splits →", "← Back to Tidelyne") | Reproduced from source (23 lines). ### Code quality, performance & SEO (code) — 12 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R4-CODE-01 | SEO / hygiene (live site) | high | robots.txt cannot work on a GitHub *project* site, and Jekyll turns the backend README into an indexable page: the backend sources, tools and repo docs are published and crawlable (R2-CODE-14's fix is a no-op) | Reproduced live: origin-root robots.txt 404, /JAC_Online/supabase/ served as a Jekyll page titled 'Supabase backend (project gzqovsffnziumfbmqdes) \| JAC_Online', README.md and tools/apply_fixes.py 200. Added _config.yml with exclude [supabase, tools, README.md, LICENSE, .gitignore, .claude] so Jekyll drops them from the build; robots.txt keeps Allow + Sitemap but its comment now says it is never fetched on a project site and the four Disallow lines are gone; README rows for robots/supabase/tools rewritten ('excluded from the published site via _config.yml') plus a new _config.yml row. Verify after push: curl -I .../JAC_Online/supabase/ -> 404. Merger (× R4-COPY-27): README rows reconciled — see R4-COPY-27. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-02 | PWA install / update continuity | medium | site.webmanifest `"id": "./"` resolves to the origin root, not the app: it silently changed the installed app's identity and collides with any other project site on the same GitHub Pages origin | site.webmanifest id is now "/JAC_Online/" (the id member resolves against start_url's origin, so "./" meant https://danielswims101.github.io/; the absolute path equals the default identity existing installs already carry). README manifest note added: id must stay the absolute app path and only changes on a domain move. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-03 | PWA / every navigation | medium | sw.js serves a host 5xx (or any non-OK page) instead of the cached shell: a GitHub Pages outage shows a broken page although the whole app is precached | Reproduced with the Node harness (real sw.js in a vm): network 503 over a cached shell was served as 503. networkFirst now treats status >= 500 as a miss when a cached shell (key or index) exists, in both the race branch and the no-cache branch; 404s still pass through for the custom 404.html. Harness after: 503 -> 200 cached shell, 404 -> 404, no-cache 503 -> 503 (never the plain-text offline stub). | fixed |
| R4-CODE-04 | updates (What's New), PWA update flow, release process | medium | 44 commits of fixes are about to ship under the unchanged version 3.1.1: the sw.js cache name, `?v=3.1.1` cache-buster, What's New entry and "previous version" logic all read as "nothing changed", and README's step-2 rationale is wrong | README release step 2 rewritten: the bump changes the cache name, sweeps the previous cache, offers every tab a reload and changes the viz-human.js ?v= buster while the shell itself is network-first; a version-equality grep check was added to the checklist (verified it prints 1). The 3.1.2 UPDATES entry and sw.js VERSION bump are deliberately NOT done here: the fix rules reserve version strings for the release step. | partial |
| R4-CODE-05 | PWA / every cold launch | low | Navigation preload is not enabled: every network-first navigation waits for the service worker to boot before the request even starts | activate() enables registration.navigationPreload (guarded); networkFirst consumes event.preloadResponse before falling back to fetch(req), keeping the 3 s lie-fi race. Harness: navigationPreload enabled after activate = true; a preload response is served with 0 extra fetch() calls. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-06 | PWA offline typography / update cost | low | Offline, the installed app renders in Arial Narrow/Impact fallbacks because Google Fonts responses are never cached, while 73 KB of install-only icons are re-downloaded by every user on every version bump | sw.js routes fonts.gstatic.com through fontFileCacheFirst and fonts.googleapis.com through fontCssStaleWhileRevalidate, both in tl-fonts-cache (name outside the swept prefix), caching only ok non-opaque responses; the fresh CSS's URL list sweeps font files it no longer names. The fonts <link>s on index.html and the four static pages carry crossorigin so the CSS response is CORS (Google sends ACAO:*), not opaque. apple-touch-180.png, icon-512.png and icon-512-maskable.png dropped from PRECACHE (13 -> 10 files, -73 KB per bump). Harness: font served offline from tl-fonts-cache; browser: fonts still load with crossOrigin=anonymous on the link. The pane blocks SW registration so offline typography was proven in the harness, not the pane. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-07 | home / SEO and link previews | low | The unified meta description is 231 characters: Google, Slack and X truncate it around 155–200, so the snippet ends mid-list and never reaches "installable and works offline" | meta description, og:description and twitter:description are now the 155-char 'Free swim training app that works offline: 3D stroke visualizer, technique library, AI coach, pace calculators, deck timers, race planner and training log.' (differentiators first); the two JSON-LD descriptions keep the fuller list. Verified in the DOM: 155/155/155 (was 231). | fixed |
| R4-CODE-08 | PWA install discoverability (home, palette, nav) | low | The install prompt is captured on every page but can only be triggered from the What's New page: on Android the browser's own install banner is suppressed and the site offers no replacement anywhere else | Added a conditional palette quick action 'Install Tidelyne as an app' (when: window.__tlCanInstall, evaluated at query time via a new available() filter in search/render/recents) and an 'Install app' control in the home footer links (button styled as a link, hidden by default); renderFooterInstall() sets the flag and shows the control while a prompt is held and not standalone, or an 'Add to Home Screen' link to the What's New card on iOS Safari; tlFooterInstall() prompts or navigates. Verified in the pane with a synthetic beforeinstallprompt: footer control and palette action appear, and both disappear after the prompt is consumed. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-09 | updates (Install card) | low | `isIOS()` returns true for an Android UA when `navigator.platform` is 'MacIntel' with touch points: the What's New card then hides the Install button and tells an Android user to "tap Share, then Add to Home Screen" | Reproduced at 375x812 (Android UA, platform MacIntel, 5 touch points): the card showed the iOS instructions. isIOS() now returns false for an Android UA or a userAgentData.platform of Android/Windows/Linux/Chrome OS, and the iPadOS-13 heuristic additionally requires a Safari-engine UA. After: the same emulation shows the generic 'not offered yet' message and the Install button logic runs. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-10 | all (nav, .noise), visualizer (lab overlays) | low | Invisible effects with a per-frame cost: a full-viewport feTurbulence overlay at 2.5 % opacity, a 97 %-opaque nav that still blurs everything under it on every scroll frame, and blurred overlays on top of the live WebGL canvas | .noise rule, its markup and its print-rule entry removed; nav background rgba(4,13,24,0.985) with backdrop-filter dropped; .vos-item and .viz-phase-bar solid rgba(4,13,24,0.92) without blur; the two containing-block comments reworded (the fixOffset guard is kept: one getBoundingClientRect on drawer open, cheap and defensive). Verified computed styles: nav backdrop-filter none, no .noise element, overlay blur none. Static change; frame timing cannot be measured in the hidden pane. Merger (× R4-VISUAL-08): see R4-VISUAL-08. | fixed |
| R4-CODE-11 | n/a (code health) | low | Duplicated helpers inside the one script: the SSE reader loop is pasted four times, the clipboard fallback three times, the body-scroll lock twice, `$`/`setText`/`fmtDate` twice or more | One top-level async readAIStream(resp, guard, onText) replaces the four identical SSE loops (motion director, coach chat, workout generator, visualizer analysis); the chat/generator keep partial answers via onText. One window.tlCopyText(text) -> Promise<boolean> (Clipboard API then hidden-textarea execCommand) replaces the planner's fallbackCopy and is the first attempt for the timers and analytics copies, which keep their bespoke visible-selection last resorts. Verified in the pane: multi-chunk stream assembles 'Hello world', non-text deltas/bad JSON ignored, an AbortError mid-stream still leaves the partial text with the caller, tlCopyText resolves a boolean. NOT done: merging the two scroll locks - they are different strategies (the drawer's iOS fixed-body lock with scroll restore vs the palette's overflow lock with gutter compensation), not copies; $/setText/fmtDate are module-private inside separate IIFEs. Merger (× R4-FUNCTION-01): readAIStream(resp, guard, onText, stale) — see R4-FUNCTION-01. | partial |
| R4-CODE-12 | n/a | low | Stale / dead bits still shipped: two one-shot migration scripts from August, comments citing a `test/run.js` harness that is not in the repo, an unused colour helper, a dead CSS selector, a stale colour fallback and mismatched favicon `sizes` | tools/apply_fixes.py and tools/rename.py deleted (git keeps them) and the README tools row updated; the five 'node test harness / test/run.js' comments reworded to 'exposed for ad-hoc console/Node checks'; unused srgbToLinear removed from viz-human.js (linearToSrgb, which is used, stays); .workout-phase h4 dropped from the screen and print rules; analytics --white4 fallback is #7b95ad; favicon.ico sizes="16x16 32x32" on 404/privacy/terms/disclaimer. | fixed |

### Swimming domain accuracy (domain) — 18 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R4-DOMAIN-01 | visualizer (3D lab, all strokes — breaststroke worst) | medium | every phase name/description is written for the keyframe instant at the start of the phase, but the panel shows it for the whole interval until the next keyframe, during which the rig is already doing the *next* thing. With five long breaststroke phases the panel is wrong for most of the cycle: during "Glide & Streamline" (u 0–0.30, desc "the body rides… | Reproduced (25-sample sweep: 'Glide & Streamline' shown while hands went 0.14→1.11 m apart, 'Out-Sweep' during knee 16→121°, 'Heel-Draw' during knee 123→7°). updateSwimmer now labels the NEAREST keyframe (li = localT<0.5 ? idx : nextIdx) and phaseIdxFromClock3D uses the same rule so arrow-key stepping matches the panel; jumpToPhase3D lands a hair inside a phase so tapping a step is unchanged. Verified: out-sweep now labelled 'Out-Sweep & Catch' (u 0.16–0.38), in-sweep/heel-draw 'In-Sweep…' (0.40–0.52), the kick 'Squeeze into the Glide' (0.56–0.68); tap-to-jump 0–4 → label 0–4; at 70% through phase 1 the panel says Phase 3 and ArrowRight goes to Phase 4. Fixes all five strokes and the AI/fallback motions. | fixed |
| R4-DOMAIN-02 | home (AI card) + ai (welcome) | medium | the AI coach receives no site content at all. The request body is `{ mode, messages: conversationHistory }` (index.html ~line 5773) and the ask-ai function's `coach` system prompt is a 60-word generic "experienced competitive swimming coach" instruction with none of the site's numbers (10-second HR counts, Z2 = 2–3 mmol, sprint taper 14–21 days, the set… | Reproduced (sendMessage body is {mode, messages} only). Home AI card bullet → 'Covers the same ground as this site — sets, zones, taper, race plans — from general coaching knowledge, so check its numbers against the pages here'; AI welcome adds 'I answer from general coaching knowledge, not from this site's pages — for Tidelyne's own zone numbers and taper windows, use the Zones and Taper pages' (linked). Verified: thrust peak 104 at u 0.20, cycle mean net +1.8 N, tempo and poses untouched. | fixed |

**Round 4 totals:** 108 findings — 104 fixed, 2 partial, 2 deferred, 0 not reproduced. Partial: R4-CODE-04 and R4-CODE-11 (see their rows). Deferred: R4-COPY-29 and R4-DOMAIN-04 (the 3.1.2 release note — reserved for the release step). Post-merge fix commit: "forensic r4: post-merge fix" (formatted dates in the log heading and delete notice; AI welcome fits the phone pane).

## Round 5 — 2026-09-18

Round focus: fifth full sweep of 3.1.1 after the round-4 merge (048fd19) — each lens re-checked the round-1–4 fixes for regressions, then hunted edge cases and resilience: long or hostile stored data (unbroken tokens, URLs, corrupt or partial log entries, oversized backups, 500-character names), phone widths down to 320 px, keyboard-only and screen-reader paths through the timers, log undo and the planner, double-taps and re-entry on every async button, the service worker with a broken CacheStorage, and the physics tables of the 3D lab (sign of net force vs velocity on every stroke and the fallback motions). Fixes were made on six branches (forensic/r5-*) and merged into main with conflicts resolved to keep every lens's intent; the two independent log-paging implementations (R5-VISUAL-07 / R5-FUNCTION-17) and the four log-entry coercion fixes (R5-COPY-17 / R5-FUNCTION-11 / R5-A11Y-10 / R5-CODE-05) were unified into one logEntryHTML() + coerceLogFields() path, and the two router fixes (R5-FUNCTION-13 / R5-CODE-09) into one rule. Post-merge: check_index.py PASS against the pre-merge baseline (no new duplicate top-level names), node --check on viz-human.js and sw.js, version strings untouched (3.1.1), all 22 pages open without console errors at desktop and 375 px, the human rig builds and all five strokes render distinct animated poses, drawer and palette open/close, the log (garbage entries, paging, delete/undo, double-save and rate guards, field-mirrored notices), router deep links, formatTime/parseTime, Golf Score bands and limits, Pace Zones floor and scroll region, send-off did-you-mean and world-record warning, tempo/split announcements, away-pause on return, planner fit/splits/meet and heading levels, Updates outline, Supabase lazy load and the auth-gate error focus were all verified in the browser. One post-merge fix on main (843eea1): coerceLogFields keeps the text fields string-only.

### Function & data (function) — 19 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R5-FUNCTION-01 | log | medium | Training Log: a double-tap on Save logs a second, blank session | saveLogEntry(): ignores a repeat within 600 ms of the last successful save (_lastLogSaveAt) and refuses an all-blank session that matches the one just saved (same date/type/zone, within 15 s) with 'Looks like this session was just saved.' Verified: two click()s in one tick -> 1 logged; a blank click 700 ms later -> refused; a real second session saves. (The button is not toggled disabled: doing that synchronously blurs the focused button, and the whole save path is synchronous so the timestamp guard is what prevents the duplicate.) | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-02 | log | medium | Training Log: a long unbroken note (a pasted URL) makes the whole page scroll sideways | .log-content gets min-width:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere and .log-title/.log-meta/.log-notes overflow-wrap:anywhere. Verified: 330-char URL note + 200-char main set -> document scrollWidth 743 at a 748 viewport (was 2605) and 375 at 375 px. Merged with R5-COPY-01 / R5-VISUAL-01. | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-03 | profile | medium | My PRs: when storage is full or blocked, PRs and the name silently vanish | savePR() commit(): a failed lsSet shows 'Could not save this PR — your device storage may be full or blocked.' in #pr-hint and sets aria-invalid on the field; saveName(): a failed lsSet routes to tlNotice('profile-notice', …, 'profile-name') and the header keeps the last stored name; the notice clears on the next successful save. Verified with setItem throwing QuotaExceededError, and recovery once storage works again. | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-04 | timers (Pace Clock › Send-off) | medium | Send-off calculator accepts "1.30" as a 1.3-second interval (and "5" as 5 s) | parseMSS(): when window.parseTime exists its verdict is final (the local regex is only the stand-in for a missing parseTime, and now also floors at 10 s); tlt_sendoff shows 'Enter the interval as m:ss — did you mean 1:30?' for a single-dot/comma value (same for the pace field). Verified: '1.30' and '5' are refused with the table hidden; '1:30', '90', '0:45', '1:07,5' still work. | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-05 | planner (Race Predictor › Fit) | low | Race Predictor "fit your exponent": an error leaves the previous fit (and "Saved") on screen | runFit(): a local fail(msg) sets the error and empties #pl-fit-out; every early return uses it. Verified: 48.00/1:44.00 -> card; then 1:30.00 -> error with #pl-fit-out empty (was the stale '1.115 … Saved' card). | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-06 | every page (command palette) | low | ⌘K "Start tempo trainer" only opens the Timers page | 'Start tempo trainer' quick action gets an after(): clicks the Tempo tab button when not active, calls tlt_tempoToggle() unless the trainer is already running (button has .pause), and focuses #tlt-tempo-start. Verified via ⌘K 'tempo' + Enter: hash #timers, Tempo tab active, button reads 'Pause', status '3-2-1, then 83 strokes/min.', focus on the button (audio verified with a fake AudioContext because the hidden pane's real audio clock is stalled). | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-07 | every page (command palette) | low | ⌘K empty query lists recently used actions twice | render() empty-query branch: keys already shown under Recent are excluded from Actions and Pages. Verified: 3 recents -> 30 results with no duplicate rows (was 33 with three repeats). | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-08 | planner (Split Planner) | low | Split Planner: a blank fade or start-bonus field is silently treated as 0 and remembered | runSplits() reads the raw fade/bonus strings: a blank bonus fails with 'Enter the start bonus in seconds (0 to 3) — the LCM default is 1.4 s.', a blank fade with a negative/positive strategy fails with 'Enter the second-half difference as a percentage (−15 to 15) — 0 means even halves.'; the disabled fade for the other strategies still counts as 0; nothing is persisted on failure. Verified: blanks -> errors, tl_split_last untouched; -2 / 1.4 -> plan and persisted correctly. Merged with R5-COPY-21 (range wording). | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-09 | calculator (Golf Score) | low | Golf Score: the displayed score and its rating disagree at the band edges | calcGolf() rounds the score once (Math.round) and both displays and rates that number. Verified: 31.6+28 -> '60' High performance; 31.4+28 -> '59' World-class; 38.6+28 -> '67' Competitive. Merged with R5-COPY-14 / R5-DOMAIN-09. | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-10 | timers (Pace Clock › Split taker) | low | Split taker: a double-tap records 0.00 s laps that become "fastest" | spSplit(): a split within 0.3 s of the previous one (or of the start) is ignored. Verified: START, 400 ms, three SPLITs in one tick, 350 ms, SPLIT, STOP -> laps 0.40 / 0.35 only, no 0.00 'fastest'. Merged with R5-A11Y-04 into one spSplit(): a lap under 0.3 s is ignored and every accepted split is announced. | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-11 | log | low | Training Log renders stored garbage as "undefined", "Effort 99/10", "1000000000 min" | New coerceLogFields() (the Import rules: type/zone strings ≤40, dist/dur whole and clamped to LOG_MAX_*, effort 1–10 else 0, mainset ≤200, notes ≤1000) shared by sanitizeLogEntries() and readLog(), which normalises a dirty entry once and persists it (dates are kept as stored, never dropped); renderLog builds the meta line from present parts only and shows 'Session' for a missing type. Verified: the auditor's two garbage entries render 'zzz Session — Effort —' and '2026-02-31 Session — 600 min · Effort —', delete labels have no 'undefined', and a clean read performs no write. Merged with R5-CODE-05 / R5-A11Y-10 / R5-COPY-17: coerceLogFields() is the one rule (readLog, sanitizeLogEntries and logEntryHTML use it); the merger made its text fields string-only (post-merge fix) so an object zone never prints "[object Object]". | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-12 | log | low | Training Log never re-reads storage when the page is shown (stale after another tab writes) | window.onShow_log re-renders the list; a window 'storage' listener re-renders on swimiq_log (or a clear) and calls initProfile() for swimmer_name / pr_* keys. Verified: 2,000 entries written while on #analytics -> #log shows them on show; a dispatched StorageEvent for swimiq_log and swimmer_name updates the list and the header name. | fixed |

Merged with R5-CODE-09: an id inside another view routes to that view and focuses the element (code); an id outside every view (#nav-drawer, #tl-palette, #access-gate) still falls through to Home with the hash cleaned (function); #main unchanged. | fixed |

Press Resume to continue.'; the page note now says the interval timer keeps counting while the screen/tab is hidden and that opening another Tidelyne page pauses both timers (the split taker keeps timing). Verified by starting the interval timer and navigating to #home and back. Merged with R5-A11Y-11: stopAll passes the reason to itPause and sets the awayPaused flags. | fixed |

Verified: 9.01 -> '9.01s', 0.5 -> '0.50s', 10 -> '10.00s', 59.999 -> '1:00.00', 65.5 -> '1:05.50'. Merged with R5-CODE-08 (non-finite / sub-0.005 s input returns "--"). | fixed |
| R5-FUNCTION-17 | log | low | Training Log renders every session (2,000 → 22,000 DOM nodes) with no paging | renderLog() pages the history: the newest 50 render (LOG_PAGE, _logShown) with a 'Showing 50 of 2,000 sessions' line plus 'Show 50 more' and 'Show all 2,000' buttons (delegated on #log-entries); after a click focus lands on the first newly revealed session title and the sr status says 'Showing 100 of 2,000 sessions.'; readLog() stays the source so delete/undo and the 30-day summary are unchanged. | R5-FUNCTION-19 | profile, calculator, planner, analytics, timers (every parseTime/parseMSS field) | low | Time fields reject a comma decimal ("52,30") | parseTime() normalises a trailing ',d{1,3}' to a decimal point before the existing rules (so '52,30', '1:52,30' and the phone form '1.52,30' parse); parseMSS's stand-in regex mirrors it. ### Accessibility (a11y) — 19 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R5-A11Y-01 | updates | medium | What's New: "Show 2 older releases" stays visible (and dead) after the older releases are shown — `hidden` is overridden by the button's own `display:inline-block` | Reproduced (hidden=true, computed display inline-block, 6 releases shown). Added `#page-updates .tl-upd-more[hidden]{display:none}` after the inline-block rule; after tlShowAllUpdates() the button is display:none, height 0. | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-02 | log (Session History) | medium | Training log "Undo" is a 6-second time limit, its button vanishes under keyboard focus (focus drops to `<body>`), and it lives inside the `role="alert"` region | Reproduced (notice cleared after 6.3 s, focus fell to body, Undo button inside role=alert). Undo now has no time limit: it stays until the next save, Clear All, backup import or leaving the page (onPageChange chain, `clearLogUndo()`); the Undo/Undo-all button is a sibling `<p id="log-undo">` next to the alert (flex row `.log-notice-row`, inline on desktop, wraps below on 375 px), the alert reads as plain text, the sr status says 'Undo is available until you save, clear, or leave this page.', and clearLogUndo() moves focus back into the list (focusLogAfterChange) before hiding a focused Undo. Verified: 2 deletes -> 'Undo all' -> both restored, focus on a delete button; leaving the page clears the pending undo and notice. Merged with R5-VISUAL-09 (data-field-msg kept on #log-notice) and R5-CODE-07 (importBackup still calls clearLogUndo after the size-guarded settings import). | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-03 | all (Log In / Sign Up / Reset / New password), workouts (Generate), visualizer (Generate A… | medium | Nine action buttons are set `disabled` while they hold keyboard focus, so focus is dropped to the document (Firefox/Safari) and never restored | Reproduced (Skip disabled while activeElement; auth/chat/generator buttons set disabled on the activated button). New helpers tlSetBusy(btn,on)/tlIsBusy(btn) set aria-disabled+aria-busy instead of disabled (CSS `button[aria-busy="true"]{opacity:.45;cursor:progress}`), used by doSignup/doLogin/doForgotPassword/doSetNewPassword, setAIStreaming/sendToAI, generateMotion (which also gained a re-entry guard), generateWorkout and generateVisualization, plus the panel Enter-to-submit handler. itButtons() moves focus to the Start/Run-again toggle before disabling a focused Skip. Verified in browser: Send stays activeElement while busy with aria-busy/aria-disabled set and cleared; skipping the last block leaves focus on '↺ Run again'. Merged with R5-CODE-02 / R5-CODE-13: generateMotion keeps one re-entry guard (tlIsBusy covers disabled and aria-busy) and its error branches use tlSetBusy(btn,false) plus aiServerText(err.error). | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-04 | timers (Tempo Trainer, Split taker) | medium | Tempo trainer nudges are silent while idle and the split taker never announces a recorded split or the stop time | Reproduced (idle nudge left #tlt-tempo-status empty; splits/stop/reset silent). tpSet now announces every change after a 150 ms debounce whether idle, paused or running ('65 strokes/min.', '… — beeping now.', '… — applies on Resume.') and explains a refused change at the range limit ('Fastest setting — 200 strokes/min.'); callers with their own richer message (mode switch, tap tempo, preset) pass quiet=true. Split taker: Start -> 'Running — tap Split for each split.', each split -> 'Split 2: 0:00.28 — total 0:00.61', Stop -> 'Stopped at … — 3 splits recorded. Start begins a new one.', Reset -> 'Reset — ready.' All verified in #tlt-sp-status / #tlt-tempo-status. Merged with R5-FUNCTION-15 (tempo) and R5-FUNCTION-10 (splits). | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-05 | planner (Race Predictor, Split Planner, Meet Countdown) | low | Planner headings inserted at run time keep their raw level 4 while the static `<h4>` panels are exposed as level 2 — the outline skips 2 → 4 inside Race Predictor, Split Planner and Meet Countdown | Reproduced (dynamic <h4> results exposed at level 4 under level-2 panels; also found the old pass was route-dependent because the goal-group <h2>s render on show). The outline pass is now window.tlSyncHeadingLevels(page): idempotent, caches the rank map per page from the headings present at load and ranks later-rendered tags one level below the nearest smaller mapped tag. Planner result titles (predictor head, interpretation, split compare, checkpoints) are <h5> (CSS selectors updated) and the goal-group eyebrows are <h4 class="section-eyebrow">; plSyncHeadings() runs after renderGoals, renderPredict, the split compare render and renderMeet. Verified whether loaded on #planner or navigated to: 1 > 2 (panels/groups) > 3 (results), no skipped levels on any page. Merged with R5-COPY-02 (h5 rule keeps overflow-wrap:anywhere). | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-06 | updates | low | What's New: the six release titles are level 3 under "INSTALL TIDELYNE" (level 2); the release history itself has no heading | Reproduced (h4 INSTALL at level 2, six h3 releases nested under it, ol labelled by a plain string). INSTALL TIDELYNE is now an <h2> (same styling), a new `<h2 class="section-eyebrow" id="tl-upd-history-h">Release history</h2>` precedes the timeline and the <ol> is aria-labelledby it; renderTimeline re-syncs heading levels. Verified outline: L1 WHAT'S NEW, L2 INSTALL TIDELYNE, L2 Release history, L3 x6. | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-07 | visualizer, season | low | Three remaining section titles are styled `<div>`s: Visualizer "Watch It — Real Stroke Demos", "Study It — 3D Biomechanics Lab", Season "2026–27 National Meet Calendar" | Reproduced (three div.section-eyebrow titles). 'Watch It — Real Stroke Demos', 'Study It — 3D Biomechanics Lab' and the season 'National Meet Calendar' eyebrow are <h2 class="section-eyebrow"> (existing h2.section-eyebrow styling), an sr-only h2 'Where you are in the season' labels the season card, and the live phase title became an <h3> so it nests under the lab heading. Verified: no div eyebrow without a heading remains; season table aria-labelledby points at a real heading. | fixed |
| R5-A11Y-08 | planner (Meet Countdown) | low | Meet-countdown card is `aria-live` and is rebuilt with identical markup on every planner entry and every tab-visibility return — screen readers re-read the whole card each time | Reproduced (1 identical childList replacement on every return to the planner; card was aria-live). aria-live removed from #pl-mt-card; renderMeet writes the card/checkpoints only when the generated markup changed (setMeetHtml cache) and returns a one-line summary that Save announces via #pl-mt-status ('Saved — Winter Juniors: 83 days to go — Pre-taper — full training.'); Clear announces 'Cleared — no meet set.' Verified: 0 mutations on leaving and returning. | fixed |

Verified with [{id:1},{id:3,date:'garbage',type:'',zone:null},…]: 'Session — Unknown date', 'Delete session Unknown date Session', markup still escaped. Merged with R5-FUNCTION-11 / R5-CODE-05 / R5-COPY-17: type falls back to "Session", date to "Unknown date"; a blank zone is omitted rather than shown as "—". | fixed |

(or 'Interval timer', or both). Verified sequence in #tl-ai-status: 'Home page', 'Swim Timers page', 'Tempo trainer paused while you were away — press Resume.' Merged with R5-FUNCTION-14. | fixed |

### Code quality, performance & SEO (code) — 16 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R5-CODE-01 | all (service worker) | high | sw.js: a broken CacheStorage takes the whole site offline (every navigation and asset request rejects) | Reproduced with the auditor's Node harness (caches.open rejecting: navigation, navigation+preload and asset all REJECTED). sw.js: networkFirst and staleWhileRevalidate now end in .catch(() => storageBroken(event, req)), which answers from event.preloadResponse when the browser already started the request, else fetch(req), and unregisters the worker once so the next load runs worker-free. Harness after: 200 <html>live</html> / 200 <html>preloaded</html> / 200 for the asset, with registration.unregister called once. node --check passes. | fixed |
| R5-CODE-02 | visualizer (AI motion director) | medium | 3D lab "Reconstruct" has no in-flight guard: Enter three times = three concurrent ask-ai requests (and fires mid-IME) | Reproduced in the browser (fetch/sb/currentUser stubbed): three synthetic Enters plus one isComposing Enter on #motion-prompt -> 4 ask-ai calls while #motion-btn was already disabled. generateMotion() now returns when btn.disabled; the inline onkeydown attribute is gone and a listener beside the #ai-input one uses tlPlainEnter(e) (no IME confirm, no Shift/Alt/Ctrl/Cmd+Enter). After: 3 plain Enters + IME Enter + Shift+Enter -> 1 call; an idle IME Enter fires nothing. Merged with R5-A11Y-03 / R5-A11Y-15 (see those rows). | fixed |
| R5-CODE-03 | all (first load) and every version bump (both files are precached) | medium | ~70 KB gzipped of source comments ship to every visitor (index.html 39 KB gz, viz-human.js 32 KB gz) | Reproduced on the current files (gzip -9): index.html 237,517 B -> 196,447 B without full-line comments (-41 KB), viz-human.js 105,452 -> 72,856 (-32.6 KB), sw.js 4,709 -> 2,594; ~76 KB gz per first visit. Not fixed: the remedy is a deploy-time minification pipeline (GitHub Actions with html-minifier-terser/terser and the Pages source switched to GitHub Actions), which is a build step, new tooling and a repository setting only the owner can change, all outside this round's fix rules; the no-pipeline alternative (moving thousands of lines of maintainer notes into README) is a wholesale rewrite, not a surgical fix. Recommended: a .github/workflows/pages.yml that copies the repo to dist/, runs html-minifier-terser --remove-comments --minify-js --minify-css on index.html and terser --comments false on viz-human.js and sw.js, keeps _config.yml's exclusions, then upload-pages-artifact + deploy-pages; verify with curl \| gzip \| wc -c. | deferred |
| R5-CODE-04 | all (first load) | medium | supabase-js (218 KB, 55 KB gz) is downloaded and parsed on every page for every visitor, although only a signed-in session or a Log In / AI click needs it | Reproduced: cold #home with no session downloaded supabase.js (encodedBodySize 55,685) and created a client. The static <script> tag is replaced by loadSupabase() in the head (same src, integrity, crossorigin, async, onload/onerror -> onSupabaseScript), called at boot only when an sb-*-auth-token key exists or the hash is an auth callback (isAuthCallbackHash moved to the head, one definition); otherwise from authUnavailable()/openAuthGate() (every AI entry point and the nav Log In button pass through these) and from activatePage('ai'\|'workouts'). sbScriptState gains 'idle'; loadSupabase flips it to 'loading' so the existing waiter path opens the dialog when the library lands. Verified: #home stays idle with 0 script tags; Log In click -> loading -> dialog opens on #login-email with the library fetched; a stored sb-…-auth-token key loads it from the head at boot; #ai warms it; a blocked-CDN simulation still marks the nav button aria-disabled and opens the overlay with AUTH_NOLIB_MSG. The preconnect is kept. | fixed |
| R5-CODE-05 | log | medium | Training log renders "undefined", "null" and "[object Object]" for stored sessions with missing or wrong-typed fields | Reproduced with the auditor's swimiq_log: list items read 'undefined', 'null', '[object Object]', '12 x'. readLog()'s map now coerces every field the way sanitizeLogEntries does (type/zone/mainset/notes string-only and capped at 40/40/200/1000, dist/dur rounded positive ints capped at LOG_MAX_DIST/LOG_MAX_DUR, effort 1–10 else 0, date string sliced to 10 chars) and sets dirty so the cleaned list is written back once; renderLog falls back to 'Swim' for an empty type and omits an empty zone segment. After: no 'undefined'/'[object' in the list, the stored entries are normalised, a second readLog() writes nothing; layout checked at 375 px (no overflow). Merged with R5-FUNCTION-11 (coerceLogFields) — the type fallback is "Session" (function/a11y) rather than "Swim"; the date rule from this lens (string or number only) is kept in readLog. | fixed |

## Round 6 — 2026-09-18

Round focus: sixth full sweep of 3.1.1 after the round-5 merge (ccdc4b7) — each lens re-checked the round-1–5 fixes for regressions and then went after what the earlier rounds had not measured: case-sensitive unit symbols under uppercase labels, palette title-casing, the phase strip's hint, duplicated footnotes and inconsistent option names (copy); the AI page's title scale and phone chat budget, line length on the wide pages, the timers' above-the-fold order, the empty lab canvas before the swimmer builds, the tablet quick-prompt strip, orphaned grid cards, ad-hoc primary-button sizes and nav gaps at the breakpoints (visual); keyboard focus after the drawer's palette, the 30-day log window and its averages, '−0%' deltas, sub-world-record predictions, two YouTube players at once, the renderer's pixel ratio after a display change, import notices lost to the reload, oldest-first backups over the 2,000 cap and hostile stored PRs (function); phone-stacked tables losing their column headers, the tempo pulse above three flashes a second, text-only scaling to 200 %, dropdown descriptions inside link names, forced-colors mode and alert-vs-status on the log (a11y); the service worker's font sweep across two stylesheets, rendering while the lab is off-screen or paused, touch-action on the canvas, synthesised bold, preconnect credentials, a 67-character route title, 13 KB of maintainer prose in viz-human.js, safe-area insets and social-preview alt text (code); and the workout generator's volume targets and prompt labels, breaststroke/dolphin/freestyle thrust timing against the rig's own limb speed, an undefined Efficiency readout, pacing percentages, the UW drill depth, the hip return-to-kick phase, the flip-turn distances, dryland safety framing, the tempo trainer's per-unit limits and two technique-page physics claims (domain). Fixes were made on six branches (forensic/r6-copy 23, -visual 20, -function 17, -a11y 6, -code 11, -domain 15) and merged into main one by one; 7 conflict hunks (function 1, a11y 1, code 2, domain 2 + viz-human.js 1) were resolved by hand keeping every lens's intent — the cross-references are in the Fix column. Post-merge: check_index.py PASS against the pre-merge baseline (no new duplicate top-level names; 14 new names incl. vizLoading, requestRender3D, observeVizInView, tlWarmLab, tpLimits, TP_FLASH_MAX), node --check viz-human.js and sw.js, version strings untouched (sw.js VERSION = UPDATES[0].version = footer = 3.1.1). Browser smoke of the merged site (own tab, served copy on :8655, desktop / 768 / 375): all 22 pages open with no console errors beyond the pane's known service-worker registration failure; the 3D lab builds (58k-triangle human, loading overlay gone once ready) and all five strokes switch, press their button and render a lit frame, the phase jump lands on 'Phase 3: Downbeat Peak', a paused idle lab draws 0 frames and a playing one 5/5, resizeViz3D keeps canvas width = clientWidth × DPR, touch-action is pan-y pinch-zoom, applyPatches carries the breaststroke peak sub-key and the renamed butterfly phases; the drawer and ⌘K palette open and close with focus returning to the burger; the tempo trainer round-trips 200 spm → 100 cpm → 200 spm without clamping and shows the flash note only above 180; the log saves (role=status), deletes with the undo sentence, restores, and prints '—' for an unrated average; the planner shows '3 weeks 3 days', rejects a meet three years out, normalises '51.0' → '51.00'; the videos page keeps one player at a time; an invalid stored PR is flagged aria-invalid and the import notice survives the reload; the workout prompt (stubbed session and fetch, no live call) reads 'Level: Elite / National Level … Volume target: 4800-6300m'; the stacked Sets thead is a 1×1 clipped box with its three columnheaders; 200 % text at 768 causes no overflow; the four static pages carry viewport-fit=cover, the crossorigin preconnect and the summary_large_image cards. No post-merge fix was needed.
