When nothing was restored the notice is non-ok: 'That backup holds no PRs, sessions or settings to restore — nothing was changed.' (or notes skipped items / that replaced device data was cleared). | fixed |

### Accessibility (a11y) — 6 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R6-A11Y-01 | sets (all six set tables), race (Event Plans table), season (2026–27 meet calendar) at ≤ 480 px | medium | Phone-stacked tables drop their column headers from the accessibility tree (Sets ×6, Race event plans, Season meet calendar) | Reproduced at 375px (sets thead computed display:none, td::before 'Details'; #season-meet-table thead display:none). In the <=480px block both thead{display:none} rules are now the clip/sr-only pattern privacy.html uses (position:absolute;1x1;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0 0 0 0)), so the th scope=col columnheaders stay in the accessibility tree; verified thead is a 1x1 box at the table top with no layout shift or horizontal overflow. To avoid double reading, an @supports (content:"a" / "b") block gives the printed data-label (.tl-stack td::before) and the season table's 'Dates: '/'Course: '/'Status: ' prefixes empty alt text (content:… / ""), leaving the plain declarations as the fallback. Note: the season table already had sr-only Dates/Course prefixes in this revision; only the thead was missing. | fixed |
| R6-A11Y-02 | timers (Tempo Trainer tab) | medium | Tempo trainer's visual pulse can flash more than three times per second (181–200 bpm), above the 2.3.1 general flash threshold | Reproduced by code (slider max 200, every beat adds .on for 90 ms => 3.33 full-luminance flashes/s). tpFrame now lights the disc only when TP.bpm<=TP_FLASH_MAX (180) or, above that, on every other beat (accent beats only when an accent is set); the beep audio and the beat number are unchanged. Node harness over the extracted tpFrame: 200 bpm 10101010 (max 2 flashes in any 1 s window, was 4), 200 bpm + accent 3 A00A00A00, 180 bpm unchanged. A hidden .tlt-sub2 note (#tlt-tempo-flash) appears under the readout above 180 explaining the every-other-beep light (verified toggling at 180/181 via tpRender). prefers-reduced-motion now replaces the cyan flash/glow with a small-luminance change (navy3 -> card3, white3 border; gold border for accents) instead of only removing the transform. Merger (× R6-DOMAIN-11): TP_FLASH_MAX sits beside the domain lens's tpLimits(); tpRender applies the per-unit slider min/max and then toggles #tlt-tempo-flash. The cap is in beeps per minute, so it never triggers in cycles/min (max 100). Verified: 200 spm shows the every-other-beep note, cpm hides it, 60 spm hides it. | fixed |
| R6-A11Y-03 | all (header), analytics (Weekly numbers cards) | medium | Header breaks under text-only scaling: at 150 % (Android "Text scaling", Firefox/Safari text zoom) the phone header overflows and clips the menu button, at 200 % the burger is off-screen and the desktop links slide under the right-hand pills | Reproduced with html{font-size:150%/200%} at 375px (doc 388/462px, burger at 347–387 / 421–461). nav is min-height:56px (52 on phones) instead of a fixed height; the nav bundle gained navCramped/fitNav/watchFit: it measures nav.scrollWidth vs clientWidth (and, on desktop, whether the link row wrapped) via ResizeObserver on nav/.nav-brand/.nav-right/.nav-links, fonts.ready and load, and adds .tl-tight (wordmark display:none — the mark plus the link's aria-label keep the home link named) then .tl-tight2 (the new .nav-ai-label span clipped sr-only, the ✦ stays, min-width:40px target; the button keeps its name). Desktop .nav-links now flex-wraps (min-width:0) so tall text grows the bar instead of crossing the pills; the top-level link padding is 4px 10px 3px so the bar stays exactly 56px at 100%. Verified after: 375px 150%/200% burger at 323–363 with no page overflow (150% hides the wordmark; 200% too), 320px 200% adds .tl-tight2 (AI pill icon-only) and the bar still fits, 1280px 200% links 146–912 vs pills 920–1243 (no overlap, single row, bar 58px), 100% text unchanged at 375/768/1280 (no class, 52/52/56px), open drawer does not trigger the class. Analytics .an-periods tracks are repeat(3\|2,minmax(0,1fr)) and .an-period-rows strong lost white-space:nowrap (text-align:right); at 640px/200% the document stays 640px. The same minmax(0,1fr) fix went to the home .metrics-row grids, which were the remaining 200% overflow on the home page (doc 438px -> 375px), plus overflow-wrap:anywhere on .metric-card .sub. Merger (× R6-VISUAL-08): the .an-periods track is repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr)) with the visual lens's odd-last-card span rule on top; verified at 768 and 200 % text (document 763/768, no overflow). | fixed |
| R6-A11Y-04 | all (desktop nav ≥ 900 px — Learn / Tools / Track / Compete menus, 19 links) | low | Desktop dropdown links carry their one-line description inside the accessible NAME (via CSS ::after), so the same link is "Technique" on phones and "Technique Every stroke, turn and start, phase by phase" on desktop | Reproduced (getComputedStyle(#nav-technique,'::after').content = the description at 1280px). The 19 dropdown links now carry the description as real markup: <a … aria-describedby="nav-x-d">Label<span class="nav-desc" id="nav-x-d" aria-hidden="true">…</span></a>; data-desc and the ::after rule are gone, .nav-desc takes the ::after styling and is display:none below 900px as before. aria-hidden keeps the span out of the name (name-from-content) while aria-describedby still reaches it, so the link is 'Technique' at every width with the sentence as its description. Verified: ::after none, span visible in the open menu with the same size/colour, link height unchanged (50px), ids unique, the accessibility tree read names the link 'Technique'. | fixed |
| R6-A11Y-05 | visualizer (stroke, view, model, skin, speed, Forces, phase bar), ai (mode chips), timers (Strokes/Cycles seg), analytics (Session type/Zone, Distance/Minutes segs), all tabbed pages (active tab underline) | low | No forced-colors support: every selected / pressed state is drawn with box-shadow rings and alpha tints, which Windows High Contrast strips | Reproduced (grep forced-colors = 0). Added one @media (forced-colors:active) block at the end of the stylesheet: [aria-pressed=true], the timers/analytics seg buttons (selectors repeated at their original specificity so they win by order), .viz-btn.active, .ai-mode-btn.active, .phase-step.active, nav .nav-menu a.active and .nav-ai-btn.active get forced-color-adjust:none with background:Highlight/color:HighlightText/border-color:Highlight and box-shadow:none (descendants color:inherit, including the tlt-seg small), pressed toggles also get a '✓ ' ::before with empty alt text so the state survives without colour and is silent for AT; tabs (.tab-btn.active / [aria-selected=true]) get a 3px Highlight underline, current-page top links and open group buttons a 2px Highlight underline, palette options a Highlight outline, and .an-swatch/.zone-fill/.an-zone-seg/.tlt-pulse.on keep their data colours via forced-color-adjust:none. The in-app browser cannot emulate forced colours, so this is verified by parse (the media rule reports 7 rules, alt-text syntax accepted) and reasoning rather than a Windows High Contrast screenshot. | fixed |
| R6-A11Y-06 | log | low | Training-log successes ("Session saved", "Session deleted", "Session restored") are pushed through a role="alert" region and, on delete, announced a second time by the polite #log-status region | Reproduced (after save #log-notice role=alert 'Session saved — 1 logged.'; after delete the alert plus #log-status 'Session deleted. Undo is available…'). tlNotice now sets role=status when the message is kind 'ok' and role=alert otherwise (before writing the text, only on elements already carrying alert/status), so successes are polite and errors interrupt on every form that uses it (the profile notice's errors become alerts too). The delete notice carries the undo sentence itself ('Session deleted — Fri, Sep 18, 2026 · AM Pool. Undo is available until you save, clear, or leave this page.') and deleteLogEntry/undoLogDelete no longer write the duplicate to #log-status; clearLogUndo's 'deleted — ' match still clears it. Verified in the browser: validation error -> role=alert with aria-invalid/aria-describedby and the inline field message intact; save -> role=status; delete -> role=status, undo visible, #log-status empty for the event; undo -> role=status 'Session restored.' | fixed |

### Code quality, performance & SEO (code) — 11 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R6-CODE-01 | PWA / service worker (fonts cache) | medium | a Privacy / Terms / Disclaimer visit evicts the app's DM Mono and italic font files from tl-fonts-cache | sw.js sweepFontFiles now builds its keep-list from the fresh CSS plus every stylesheet already in tl-fonts-cache (cache.keys → match ignoreVary → text), so a privacy/terms/disclaimer/404 CSS refresh no longer evicts the app-only DM Mono and italic files. Reproduced with the auditor's Node harness against the unfixed sw.js (dmmono/a.woff2 gone after the legal visit); after the fix it survives the legal visit and the app refresh, and a simulated font-version change (a → a2) still drops the retired file. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-02 | visualizer (perceived performance / battery) | medium | the 3D render loop draws 75 k triangles with shadows every frame while the canvas is below the fold or the scene is paused and idle | Three parts, no swimmer retune: (1) one IntersectionObserver on #viz3d-container and #viz-info-panel — animate3D does no work while both are off-screen, and updates pose + readouts without renderer.render while only the panel is in view (so the phase text stays live when a reader scrolls to it); (2) on-demand rendering when paused — requestRender3D() (a 150 ms work window, long enough for the damping tail and the 10 Hz readout throttle) is called from jumpToPhase3D, rebuildSwimmer/showHumanRig/showMannequin (async rig arrival), x-ray/tone/forces/play toggles, setView3D's immediate jump and tween end, zoomView3D, resizeViz3D, startViz3DLoop and the observer; the loop skips when !playing, the window has passed, no tween runs and controls.isSettled() (\|Δθ\|+\|Δφ\|<1e-4, \|scale−1\|<1e-4); (3) the head OrbitControls reuses one scratch Vector3 in updateSpherical/update/dolly. Verified by driving animate3D by hand in the served copy with renderer.render counted: off-screen playing 0/10 frames, in view 10/10, panel-only 0 draws with the clock advancing, paused+idle 0/20, paused+phase jump 5/5 then 0 (panel showed 'Phase 3: Pull'), paused+drag 106 frames while the damping settled then 0, paused+resize 3/3; GL buffer read back non-black (mean luminance 100, 25 % lit). | fixed |
| R6-CODE-03 | visualizer (scroll performance on phones) | low | the 3D canvas has no touch-action, so every vertical swipe over it waits on a non-passive touchmove handler running on the same thread as the render loop | #viz3d-container gets touch-action:pan-y pinch-zoom (the JS direction lock stays as the fallback). Computed touch-action verified in the 375 px emulation; synthetic touch drags: vertical → no preventDefault, camera unchanged (0.00 movement); horizontal → orbit still works (3.18 units). The compositor fast path itself cannot be observed from the hidden pane. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-04 | all pages (typography / perceived quality) | low | bold text is synthesised: the Google Fonts request never loads a 700 weight, yet 52 <strong>, 67 table headers and the auth buttons render at 700 | DM Sans 0,700 added to both font URLs in index.html and wght@400;600;700 on privacy/terms/disclaimer/404 (variable font, no extra file). DM Mono is never asked for 700 any more: .data-table th and .footer-col h4 get font-weight:500, .zone-fill / #page-analytics .an-zone-seg / .skip-link / .msg-avatar (it is DM Mono, not DM Sans) and the four auth buttons go 700 → 500. Before: document.fonts had DM Sans 300–600 only and 85 DM Mono elements computed at 700; after: DM Sans 700 face present, 0 DM Mono elements at 700, the 53 <strong> now use a real 700 face. Not touched: the 156 Bebas Neue display headings whose UA-default bold is synthesised too (Bebas has one weight) — that is the site's established heading look and a visual-lens call. Merger (× R6-COPY-01): see R6-COPY-01 — both edits to the .data-table th rule kept. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-05 | all pages (first paint) | low | preconnect to fonts.googleapis.com is opened in credentialed mode but the stylesheet is fetched with crossorigin, so the warmed socket is never used | crossorigin added to the fonts.googleapis.com preconnect on index.html, privacy.html, terms.html, disclaimer.html and 404.html so it matches the anonymous preload/stylesheet request. Verified as a source fix (Resource Hints credentials-mode matching); connection reuse is not observable from the hidden pane. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-06 | calculator (SEO / tab strip) | low | regression of R2-CODE-16: the Pace Calculators route title is 67 characters again | PAGE_TITLES.calculator → 'Pace Calculators — Golf, Splits, Zones & Pools' (57 with the brand suffix; pageLabel() splits on ' — ' and is unaffected). A comment above PAGE_TITLES states the ≤ 60 rule and README 'Maintainer notes' gains a one-line node check next to the duplicate-id check; the check passes (max 60 = analytics). Live document.title per route re-measured: max 60. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-07 | visualizer asset (payload hygiene, viz-human.js) | low | 13 KB of maintainer prose ships as runtime string literals (notes: / sources:) that nothing reads, beside comments citing six documents not in the repo | The ten notes:/sources: string literals (13,196 chars) in viz-human.js STROKE_PATCHES are now // comment blocks inside each patch; the section header and format comment describe them as comments, and the file header explains that 'SPEC §n', 'RESEARCH §n' and 'technique-*.md' citations are the maintainer's private notes (see README 'Maintainer notes') and that the three module versions are the sections of this one file. Verified: node --check passes, JSON.stringify(TL_HumanMotion.STROKE_PATCHES) is byte-identical to the old object with notes/sources stripped, module VERSIONs unchanged, and a comment-stripping minifier now ships 236 KB instead of 249 KB. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-08 | visualizer (display correctness after a monitor change) | low | the renderer's pixel ratio is fixed at init; moving the window to a screen with a different DPR leaves the lab blurry (1× on Retina) or over-rendered (2× on a 1× display) | resizeViz3D re-reads min(devicePixelRatio,2) and calls renderer.setPixelRatio before setSize when it changed (and asks for one render, since setSize clears the buffer). Reproduced in place at DPR 2: the old path (ratio pinned at 1, setSize only) left a 333 px buffer for a 333 px CSS box; the fixed resizeViz3D gives 666 px = clientWidth × 2. Merger (× R6-FUNCTION-10): identical DPR re-read from both lenses collapsed to one; requestRender3D() after setSize kept. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-09 | PWA (installed app on notched phones) | low | no viewport-fit=cover / safe-area insets: the fixed close button of the sign-in dialog, the update toast, the drawer and the sticky nav ignore the notch and home-indicator areas in standalone landscape | viewport-fit=cover on index.html and the four static pages; body padding env(safe-area-inset-right/bottom/left) so in-flow content (sticky nav included) clears the notch in landscape and the home indicator; fixed chrome padded individually: .tl-upd-toast bottom:max(20px,inset-bottom), nav .nav-drawer padding top/right/bottom insets, #access-gate close button top/right max(8px,inset) with the plain 8px kept as the fallback declaration, .tl-pal-dialog phone sheet inset padding, .noscript-note bottom padding; the legal pages and 404 pad their body with max(…, inset). Verified the declarations are valid (CSS.supports true, computed values unchanged where env() is 0) and every route is error-free; rendering on a cut-out device was not exercised (no simulator session). | fixed |
| R6-CODE-10 | home / privacy / terms / disclaimer (social previews) | low | index.html has no twitter:image:alt, and the three legal pages declare twitter:card=summary with the 1200×630 landscape og.png, which that card crops to a square | index.html gains <meta name="twitter:image:alt"> after twitter:image; privacy/terms/disclaimer switch twitter:card to summary_large_image and add og:image:width/height, og:image:alt, twitter:image and twitter:image:alt for the 1200×630 og.png. Verified in the DOM of the served copy. | fixed |
| R6-CODE-11 | visualizer (perceived performance of the first lab open) | low | 480 KB of lab code (148 KB three.min.js + 331 KB viz-human.js; ≈105 KB gzipped for the module alone) starts downloading only after the route is already showing, and nothing warms it while the swimmer hovers or reads the CTA | tlWarmLab() (idempotent, skipped under navigator.connection.saveData or once the lab is loaded) injects <link rel=prefetch as=script> for three.min.js (crossorigin=anonymous, matching the loader) and ./viz-human.js?v=TL_VERSION; bound on pointerenter/focus/touchstart of #nav-visualizer, the Tools group button, the hero '3D Visualizer' CTA and the home Stroke Technique Lab card link; scheduleLabWarm() runs it from requestIdleCallback (4 s timeout, setTimeout fallback) when the home route activates and the connection is 4g / unknown and not data-saver. Verified in the served copy: after home idle both files appear in Resource Timing with initiator 'link' (148,140 B + 331,930 B) and nothing executes; the hover afterwards adds nothing; navigating to #visualizer then loads both scripts from cache in 2–3 ms; no CSP violation (as=script → script-src). | fixed |

### Swimming domain accuracy (domain) — 16 findings

| ID | Page | Severity | Finding | Fix applied | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| R6-DOMAIN-01 | workouts (generator prompt) | medium | the volume targets sent to the model for Elite and Senior are physically unswimmable at their upper ends. 8,000 m in 90 min is 1:07.5 per 100 *including every rest* — faster than the men's 1500 m world-record pace (1:08.0/100) held for 90 minutes with no rest at all. 5,000 m in 60 min and 10,000 m in 120 min are 1:12/100 including rest; Senior 6,500 m in… | Reproduced with a stubbed session+fetch (90-min Elite prompt read 'Volume target: 5500-8000m'). index.html VOL_TARGETS senior → 45:[2300,3000] 60:[3000,4000] 75:[3800,4800] 90:[4500,5800] 120:[5500,7500]; elite → 45:[2500,3300] 60:[3300,4300] 75:[4000,5300] 90:[4800,6300] 120:[6000,8000] (8,000 m in 120 min = 1:30/100 including rest). Age-group/masters unchanged. Verified: 90-min Elite prompt now 4800-6300m, 60-min Elite 3300-4300m, 120-min Senior 5500-7500m. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-02 | workouts (generator prompt) | medium | the prompt sends the raw <option value> tokens, not the labels the swimmer chose. The model receives "Focus: race-prep", "Level: elite", "Equipment: basic", "Meso: meso2-late", "Stroke: IM". "basic" carries no information (the page means "Fins + Paddles"), "all" does not say snorkel/fins/paddles/band, "meso1-late" does not say weeks 3–4, and… | Reproduced (prompt carried 'Focus: race-prep / Level: elite / Equipment: basic / Meso: meso2-late'). _generateWorkoutInner gains a local genLabel(id) that reads options[selectedIndex].text; Focus, Stroke, Level, Equipment, Meso and the level inside the volume-target sentence now use the labels while the option values still drive VOL_TARGETS/volScale/meso logic; the now-unused stroke/equip locals were dropped. Verified: '- Focus: Race Preparation / - Stroke: IM (all strokes) / - Level: Elite / National Level / - Equipment: Basic (Fins + Paddles) / - Meso: Meso II — Wks 7–8 … for this 90-minute Elite / National Level session'. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-03 | visualizer (3D lab, breaststroke physics) | low | the thrust peak is displayed after the kick has finished. Sampled on the human rig: the whip runs u 0.48→0.62 (left foot x −0.46 → −0.91 m, i.e. heels-at-buttocks to nearly extended) while thrust reads 62→154 N; at u 0.64–0.70 the legs are straight and static (foot x −0.93/−0.94, hips rising) and thrust reads 173/154 N — the cycle maximum with nothing… | Reproduced on the human rig (left ankle x −0.40 → −0.86 m over u 0.46–0.62, static from 0.62, while thrust climbed 60 → 175 N to peak at u 0.66). The proposed sixth pose keyframe was NOT inserted: the C1 spline re-tangents its neighbours and applySecondary keys the breaststroke ankle whip to phase index 2's localT (hold to 0.50, point by 0.88), so a split phase would pop the feet at u 0.58 — a swimmer re-tune the brief forbids. Instead a physics-only peak sub-key: viz-human.js In-Sweep phase carries peak {at:0.6, drag 60, thrust 175, lift 24, vel 1.60, eff 66}; Squeeze re-keyed drag 40 / thrust 60 (desc notes the peak came a moment earlier as the feet swept in); applyPatches deep-clones peak with the phase. index.html updateSwimmer's readout lerp passes A → A.peak → B (eased) when a keyframe carries a well-formed peak; poses, durations and labels untouched; validateMotion already whitelists fields so AI motions never carry one. Verified live: thrust 60 (0.46) → 149 (0.54) → 175 at u 0.58 with the foot still travelling → 117 (0.62) → 60 at 0.66 with the legs closed → 4 in the glide; foot positions identical to before. Per-keyframe cycle mean net ≈ +1 N, every velocity interval keeps its net-force sign. Merger (× R6-COPY-12): see R6-COPY-12 — the peak sub-key ships with the copy lens's degree-sign wording. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-04 | visualizer (3D lab, dolphin kick physics) | low | 50 N of thrust is keyed to the top reversal, where the feet are stationary (foot y 0.36–0.38 at u 0.92–1.00, the highest point of the kick), while the cycle minimum (12 N) sits at "Upbeat Peak" (u 0.83) with the feet still rising 0.03 m per 4 % of the cycle. R4-DOMAIN-05's own rule was "thrust keyed to the foot's reversal points, where its velocity is… | Reproduced (foot y flat at 0.33–0.34 over u 0.88–0.00 while thrust read 19/33/46/50). viz-human.js dolphin: Heels Up (Load) thrust 50→16, drag 48→40 (desc adds that the feet are momentarily still at the reversal, so thrust is near its floor); Upbeat Peak drag 56→48. Verified live: top reversal now reads 13/14/16/16 N; whip peak 104 N at u 0.20 unchanged; duration-weighted mean net −0.4 N; velocity intervals keep their signs (1.72→1.62 under −30 N, 1.62→1.66 under +12 N). Poses untouched. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-05 | visualizer (3D lab readouts) | low | the "Efficiency %" tile (and the "Effic." stat above the canvas) is never defined anywhere on the page or in the force caption. Its values peak in the passive glides (breaststroke glide 82–84 %, dolphin fallback 88 %) and are lowest in the propulsive phases (48–74 %), which is the opposite of what a physiologist reads as propelling efficiency (Toussaint:… | Reproduced (no definition of Efficiency anywhere on the page; glide 82–84 %, In-Sweep 52 %). The lab's force caption now ends: 'Efficiency is an illustrative economy index — the share of a phase's effort that becomes forward motion, so a passive streamline reads highest — not measured propelling efficiency.' Verified rendered; no overflow at 375 px. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-06 | race (Pacing Science) | low | "the first 50 is normally 1.5–3 s faster than the second" understates the site's own reference patterns for anyone slower than ~50 s. The planner's PATTERNS (100 BR / 100 FLY 46.9 / 53.1 %) give a 3.7 s fade at 1:00 and 4.3 s at 1:10; freestyle (48.1/51.9) gives 2.3 s at 1:00 and 2.7 s at 1:10. Stated in seconds the rule only holds for world-class 100s;… | Reproduced against PATTERNS (100 FR 48.1/51.9 = 3.8 % of race time; 100 BR/FLY 46.9/53.1 = 6.2 %, i.e. 3.7 s at 1:00). Pacing Science 100 m now reads: the first 50 is normally faster than the second by about 4 % of the race time in free and back (≈2 s at 50 s, 2.7 s at 1:10) and about 6 % in fly and breast (3–4 s); the freestyle rule of thumb is a second 50 more than ~10 % slower than the first (the normal pattern is ~8 %, since 51.9/48.1 = 1.079 — the auditor's '5 %' would have flagged every normal swim). | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-07 | sets (Tech Sets) | low | the UW 15 m drill prescribes "Max depth", contradicting Technique › Underwater ("Swim depth 0.5–0.6 m below surface") and the Ask-the-Visualizer prompt ("optimal depth (0.5-0.6m)"). Deeper than ~0.6 m the wave-drag saving is gone and the swimmer only adds distance on the way up. The set is also labelled "IM" although a 15 m underwater has no breaststroke… | Reproduced (Sets row said 'Max depth' vs Technique 0.5–0.6 m). Row now reads 'Race depth (0.5–0.6 m) · tight streamline · fly, back and free; a legal pullout on the breaststroke length · every session'. Verified rendered, no overflow at 375 px. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-08 | injury (Hip › Return to kick) | low | self-contradictory: "full kick volume" and "at 50 % of normal load" in the same phase. A swimmer returning from hip pain needs the unambiguous version. | Reproduced. Injury › Hip Phase 3 now: 'Full-range breaststroke kick again — start at 50% of your normal kick volume for the first week back, then build'. Verified rendered. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-09 | technique (Turns › Freestyle flip turn) | low | three distances that disagree in one sentence: "about one body length" (≈1.7–1.9 m for an adult), "1–1.5 m from head to wall", and "as the T passes under you" — the cross line of the T is 2.0 m from the end wall (World Aquatics facilities rule: lane markings end 2 m from the wall with a 1 m cross line). A swimmer who tucks with the head 1 m from the wall… | Reproduced (one body length / 1–1.5 m / 'as the T passes' in one sentence; the cross line is 2 m out). Turns card now: 'Approach at full speed and never look for the wall. Start the somersault as your head crosses the T — its cross line is 2 m from the wall — so the last stroke and tuck bring you round about a body length out.' Verified rendered. | fixed |
| R6-DOMAIN-10 | dryland (safety framing) | low | the page prescribes weighted pull-ups 4×6, trap-bar deadlifts 3×8, hang power cleans 4×6 and box jumps with no load guidance, no age note and no supervision line, for an audience the site's own AI preamble describes as "many of them 13–18". Nutrition, Taper, Mental, Injury, Timers and Planner all carry a footer note; Dryland (and Sets) carry none, and the… | Reproduced (Dryland and Sets footers were just 'Tidelyne'). Dryland footer adds 'Informational only — lifts and loads should be taught and supervised by a qualified strength coach; growth-age swimmers learn technique before adding load. Skip any exercise you have not been coached on.'; Sets footer adds 'Informational only — not coaching advice. Adapt every set to your own level and your coach's programme.' Same footer-bottom markup as Injury/Nutrition; verified at 375 px (no overflow). | fixed |
